Motivation: 已有模型合并研究多关注合并策略本身，较少考察模型在合并前采用的训练范式（如SFT与RL）如何影响合并效果。作者希望明确RL训练是否以及为何更适合模型合并。

Method: 作者系统比较RL训练与SFT训练的大语言模型在五类代表性任务上的合并表现，并结合经验实验与理论分析，研究梯度更新幅度、优化目标收敛行为、正负样本联合优化等因素对参数冲突的影响。

Result: 实验表明，RL显著降低任务间冲突，模型合并后的性能下降更小。进一步分析发现，RL的on-policy数据带来较小梯度更新，RL目标在收敛过程中减少冲突参数更新，正负样本联合优化使任务参数更新更少偏置。

Conclusion: RL训练范式天然更适合模型合并，因为它能降低知识覆盖风险、减少冲突参数更新，并引导模型进入更稳健且冲突更少的任务特定参数子空间。

Abstract: Model merging plays a crucial role in consolidating multiple specialized models into a single, unified model, especially in the era of large language models (LLMs). Recent research has primarily focused on developing strategies to enhance merging performance with the trained models, while the impact of training paradigms, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), on the effectiveness of model merging remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically explore the merging behavior of RL-trained LLMs compared to those trained with traditional SFT. Through comprehensive evaluations across five representative tasks, we find that RL significantly reduces task conflicts and results in less performance degradation after merging, making RL-trained models particularly well-suited for this process. To unearth the reasons behind the superior suitability of RL for model merging, we conduct extensive empirical experiments and theoretical analyses. Our findings highlight three key factors: (1) On-policy training data in RL control the gradient updates in a smaller magnitude, reducing the risk of overwriting existing knowledge for other tasks in the model. (2) The RL optimization objective, which favors ``\textit{enough is as good as a feast}", progressively reduces the magnitude and the number of conflict parameter updates as the model converges. (3) Joint optimization of positive and negative examples in RL steers the model towards an unbiased task-specific parameter subspace, ensuring robust performance while further preventing parameter conflicts.

</details>


### [71] [Developing and Validating the Spanish Version of the Large Language Models Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22041)
*Tran Gia Bao,Mo El-Haj,Sameha Al-Shakhsi,Antonio Garcia-Cabot,Raian Ali,Ala Yankouskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究验证了西班牙语版大型语言模型依赖量表（LLM-D12-SP），结果支持其双因素结构，并显示良好的信度与效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在组织与工作场景中的任务执行、决策支持和沟通中被广泛使用，评估用户对LLM心理依赖的可靠、跨文化验证工具变得重要。西班牙语人群中LLM使用快速增长，但相应的心理测量工具仍然不足。

Method: 研究对386名西班牙语参与者施测LLM-D12-SP。该量表包含两个维度：工具性依赖和关系性依赖。作者使用验证性因子分析检验原有双因素结构，并通过Cronbach's alpha评估内部一致性，同时进行区分效度和外部效度分析，考察其与互联网成瘾、LLM可信度感知、认知需求等变量的关系。

Result: 验证性因子分析支持原始双因素结构。量表整体内部一致性良好，总量表alpha为0.89，工具性依赖为0.86，关系性依赖为0.85。区分效度结果表明两个子维度相关但彼此不同。外部效度分析显示两个依赖维度均与互联网成瘾和LLM可信度感知呈正相关，而与认知需求相关较弱或无显著相关。

Conclusion: LLM-D12-SP是一个具有良好心理测量性质的西班牙语工具，可用于研究组织情境中用户对大型语言模型的心理依赖，并为该量表的跨语言结构稳定性提供了进一步证据。

Abstract: There is a growing need for reliable and culturally validated instruments to assess psychological dependency on large language models (LLMs), particularly as LLMs are increasingly used for task execution, decision-making, and communication in organizational and work-related settings. This need is especially relevant for Spanish-speaking populations, where LLM adoption is rapidly expanding, yet validated psychometric tools remain scarce. The present study reports the first validation of the Spanish version of the Large Language Model Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP), extending prior validations conducted in English- and Arabic-speaking samples. The LLM-D12 is a two-dimensional instrument assessing Instrumental Dependency (reliance on LLMs for performing tasks and supporting decisions) and Relationship Dependency (psychological reliance on LLMs for companionship and social interaction). A total of 386 Spanish-speaking participants (M = 28.0 years, SD = 6.1; 55% male) completed the LLM-D12-SP. Confirmatory factor analysis supported the original two-factor structure. The scale demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's alpha = 0.89 total; 0.86 Instrumental; 0.85 Relationship). Discriminant validity analyses indicated that the two subscales represent related but distinct constructs. External validation showed that both dependency dimensions were positively associated with internet addiction and perceived trustworthiness of LLMs, while showing weak or no association with need for cognition. Together with prior English and Arabic validations, these findings establish cross-linguistic support for the scale's structure and provide a psychometrically sound tool for investigating psychological aspects of LLM use in organizational contexts.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Native Multimodal Pre-Training From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22043)
*Haoyuan Wu,Aoqi Wu,Hai Wang,Jiajia Wu,Jinxiang Ou,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究原生多模态预训练在固定计算预算下的可预测扩展规律，给出模型规模、token 数与数据配比的计算最优配置，并验证其跨模态迁移收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型主要依赖纯文本预训练，难以充分感知多模态物理世界；原生多模态预训练虽可实现更深层的跨模态融合，但其 scaling law、资源分配规律和数据配比影响尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 作者在固定计算预算下训练基于 Transformer 的视觉-语言模型，考察不同模型大小、训练 token 数和语言/多模态数据配比对目标损失的影响；拟合计算律、资源分配幂律，并建模数据组成对计算律和分配指数的作用，进一步构建效率前沿；最后通过下游任务评估跨模态迁移与多模态上下文学习能力。

Result: 最小目标损失服从可预测的 compute law，计算最优模型规模和 token 数呈幂律增长；语言目标与多模态目标表现出不同扩展行为。语言资源分配规律对数据组成基本不敏感，而多模态资源分配对数据配比高度敏感；文本占比较高的数据混合在更大模型规模下才更具计算效率，并推动最优配置向更大模型容量倾斜。下游实验显示原生多模态预训练提升纯文本空间推理，并支持稳健的多模态 in-context learning。

Conclusion: 该研究为原生多模态基础模型的可预测扩展提供了经验依据，说明在扩展视觉-语言模型时需联合优化模型规模、训练 token 数和数据混合比例，尤其要针对语言目标与多模态目标采用不同的资源分配策略。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, their reliance on text-only pre-training restricts the perception of the multimodal physical world. Native multimodal pre-training avoids this limitation by training models from scratch on multimodal inputs, thereby achieving deep cross-modal integration and mitigating optimization asymmetries inherent to traditional late-fusion architectures. Despite these advantages, the scaling properties of this paradigm remain systematically uncharacterized. To address this gap, we investigate the optimal model size and token count for training a transformer-based vision-language model under a fixed computational budget. We demonstrate that minimal objective loss adheres to a predictable compute law, whereas compute-optimal model sizes and token counts scale as power laws. Notably, language and multimodal objectives manifest distinct scaling behaviors. The language allocation law is largely invariant to the composition of the data, indicating stable language learning regardless of the multimodal data ratio. Conversely, the multimodal allocation law is highly sensitive to this composition. Specifically, text-heavy mixtures become compute-efficient only at larger model scales, shifting the optimal resource allocation toward greater model capacity. Additionally, by modeling the influence of data composition on compute laws and allocation exponents, we derive an efficiency frontier specifying precise configurations of model size, token count, and data mixture. Downstream evaluations further reveal that native multimodal pre-training induces positive cross-modal transfer, thereby enhancing pure-text spatial reasoning and enabling robust multimodal in-context learning. In summary, this empirical research establishes the essential groundwork for predictably scaling multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [73] [Benchmarking Fine-tuning and Retrieval Strategies for a Multimodal Language Model on the NRC Reactor Operator Licensing Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22067)
*Isak Hwang,Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估31B开源多模态大模型在核电领域知识应用中的表现，发现“监督微调（SFT）+固定窗口RAG”在NRC反应堆操作员GFE考试上表现最佳，14套考试通过8套，接近人类80%通过标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核电行业引入大语言模型时，模型输出必须可靠地基于专业领域知识。研究动机是检验大模型是否能在高专业性、高风险的核工程知识任务中达到类似操作员资格考试的能力水平，并明确微调、检索增强与文档切分策略的作用。

Method: 研究以NRC反应堆操作员通用基础考试为基准，覆盖2015—2021年3月场次的14套GFE考试，包括压水堆与沸水堆。比较8种模型-检索配置：基础Gemma 4 31B-IT、基于Gemini蒸馏CoT推理的SFT、基于DOE Fundamentals Handbook的BM25稀疏检索RAG、RAFT，以及固定大小滑动窗口切分与结构感知切分两类检索切分策略。以80%作为通过标准。

Result: 最佳配置为SFT结合固定大小切分RAG，通过14套考试中的8套；未进行微调的配置均未通过任何考试。总体准确率为79.7%，置信区间跨越80%阈值；在压水堆题目上准确率达到80.2%。研究还发现：模型是否经过训练会影响最优切分策略，且RAFT在相同搜索环境下不如标准SFT。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在核电操作员考试类任务中，单纯依赖基础模型或检索不足以达到通过标准；监督微调与合适的RAG切分策略结合更能实现接近或达到操作员级别的能力。结果强调了领域微调、检索设计和训练状态之间的交互对高风险专业领域LLM部署的重要性。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into the nuclear power industry requires outputs grounded in domain-specific knowledge. This study evaluates a 31-billion-parameter open-weight multimodal model (Gemma 4 31B-IT) on its capacity to apply nuclear knowledge by benchmarking eight model-retrieval configurations against the U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) Reactor Operator licensing examination. We evaluate 14 Generic Fundamentals Examinations (GFE) from the 2015-2021 March sittings (seven pressurized and seven boiling water reactor exams) using the standard 80% human passing criterion. The base model is compared against configurations utilizing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on Gemini-distilled chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with BM25 sparse retrieval over the U.S. Department of Energy Fundamentals Handbook, and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT). Within the retrieval pipeline, we compare fixed-size sliding-window chunking against structure-aware chunking. The SFT configuration with fixed-size chunking RAG met the criterion on 8 of the 14 examinations, outperforming all alternatives, whereas no configuration without fine-tuning passed any. Aggregate accuracy reached 79.7%, with a confidence interval spanning the threshold, and 80.2% on PWR items specifically. Furthermore, two regularities emerged: the preferred chunking strategy reverses depending on the model's training state, and RAFT underperforms compared to standard SFT in matching search environments. These results demonstrate which combination of fine-tuning and search approaches achieves operator-level capabilities.

</details>


### [74] [FSE: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition by Fast-Slow Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22075)
*Yunan Zhang,Yang Fan,Heng Li,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FSE，一种用于持续学习命名实体识别的快慢专家增强跨度模型，通过共享快速专家筛选候选跨度、任务特定慢速专家分类，实现更好的抗遗忘与知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习命名实体识别需要模型逐步学习新实体类型，同时保留旧实体知识；现有方法存在灾难性遗忘，并且未充分利用不同任务间共享信息。

Method: 提出 Fast-Slow Experts（FSE）框架：共享 fast expert 学习 token-level links，用于快速过滤不可能的实体跨度；task-specific slow expert 仅对剩余候选跨度进行分类；同时引入长度衰减负采样策略缓解跨度类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在 OntoNotes 和 FewNERD 合成持续学习数据集上的实验表明，FSE 在 CLNER 场景中达到当前最优表现；消融实验验证各组件有效性，并显示其收敛更快，两个专家模块功能符合预期。

Conclusion: FSE 通过共享快速专家增强跨任务知识共享、通过任务特定慢速专家保持新任务适应能力，有效缓解持续学习 NER 中的遗忘与学习负担问题。

Abstract: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition (CLNER) enable models to incrementally learn new entity types without forgetting previously acquired ones. However, existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and insufficient exploitation of shared information across tasks. This paper proposes FSE, a Fast-Slow Experts enhanced span-based NER model for CLNER. The shared fast expert learns token-level links to efficiently filter out unlikely spans, while the task-specific slow expert performs span classification only on the remaining candidates. It stabilizes learning by promoting knowledge sharing across tasks and maintains plasticity by reducing learning burden at each task. A length-decay negative sampling strategy to mitigate span imbalance is also introduced. Extensive experiments on OntoNotes and FewNERD synthestic datasets demonstrate that FSE achieves state-of-the-art performance in CLNER scenarios, with effectiveness of each component, empirical evidence of faster convergence and expected functionality of both experts.

</details>


### [75] [MEUSLI: a Multilingual Projector for LLM-based ASR and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22100)
*Lorenzo Concina,Seraphina Fong,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEUSLI 是一个开放科学的多语言轻量投影器系列，将 Whisper 语音编码器与开源多语言大语言模型连接，支持 28 种语言的端到端 ASR，并可扩展到其他语音理解任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音编码器到 LLM 的轻量投影器多面向少数语言，常偏向英语，缺乏可复现、可扩展的开源多语言 SpeechLLM 基础设施。

Method: 构建 MEUSLI 投影器，将 Whisper 编码器输出的声学特征映射为 LLM 可接收的 token-level embeddings；在多语言 ASR 数据上训练，并使用持续学习技术扩展到训练中未见语言；进一步用少量任务特定监督迁移到语音翻译和主题识别。

Result: 在高资源和低资源语言上均取得较强 ASR 效果；可通过持续学习扩展到新语言；在每种语言仅需少量监督数据的条件下，也能支持多语言语音翻译和主题识别。

Conclusion: MEUSLI 为开源多语言语音理解提供了可扩展基础，证明轻量投影器能有效连接预训练语音编码器与多语言 LLM，并具备跨任务、跨语言迁移潜力。

Abstract: Lightweight projectors are an established way to connect pre-trained speech encoders with large language models (LLMs), mapping acoustic features into token-level embeddings for tasks like ASR and spoken question answering. Existing systems, however, typically only support a few languages and are often limited to English. We introduce MEUSLI, the first open-science multilingual projector family that links a Whisper encoder with open-source multilingual LLMs, enabling fully open-source end-to-end ASR in 28 European languages. MEUSLI extends prior monolingual pipelines, delivering strong results across high- and low-resource languages. Using proper continual leaning techniques, MEUSLI can be easily extended to other languages not seen in training. We further demonstrate that the MEUSLI projector can be leveraged beyond ASR, enabling multilingual speech translation and topic identification with only a few hours of task specific supervision per language. Overall, MEUSLI provides a solid foundation for multilingual speech understanding tasks, supporting scalable and inclu- sive open-source SpeechLLM

</details>


### [76] [Dynamic Commonsense Coordination for Empathetic Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22136)
*Zhengyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DCC 动态常识协调框架，用于提升共情回复生成中情绪理解与回复生成阶段的常识利用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情回复生成需要识别用户情绪并生成有同理心的回复；常识知识有助于情绪和情境推理，但现有方法通常在理解与生成阶段复用固定常识表示，难以在不同阶段动态协调和筛选常识。

Method: DCC 包含三个模块：SCE-AttnRes 通过残差式常识交互融合上下文与情境常识表示；AGCF 根据关联性过滤或降低低相关常识关系权重；ICAD 在解码过程中迭代检索常识记忆，实现生成阶段的动态常识感知。

Result: 在 Empathetic-Dialogues 基准上，DCC 相比 CEM 基线提升了情绪分类准确率和回复多样性，同时保持相近的困惑度；基于大语言模型的盲评显示其生成回复在相关性、连贯性和信息量方面更优。

Conclusion: 动态协调、筛选并在生成过程中迭代调用常识知识，有助于提升共情对话系统的情绪理解能力和回复质量。

Abstract: Empathetic Response Generation (ERG) requires models to recognize users' emotions and generate empathetic responses. Commonsense knowledge has been shown to support such reasoning, yet existing approaches typically reuse fixed commonsense representations across understanding and generation, limiting their ability to coordinate such knowledge across different stages. We propose DCC, a Dynamic Commonsense Coordination Framework with three complementary modules: residual-based commonsense interaction (SCE-AttnRes) to integrate contextual and situational commonsense representations, Association-Guided Commonsense Filtering (AGCF) to down-weight low-relevance commonsense relations, and Iterative Commonsense-Aware Decoding (ICAD) to dynamically retrieve commonsense memories during generation. Experiments on the Empathetic-Dialogues benchmark show that DCC improves emotion classification accuracy and response diversity over the CEM baseline while maintaining comparable perplexity. An LLM-based blind evaluation further demonstrates that DCC generates responses with better relevance, coherence, and informativeness. The code and implementation details will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hanabi-Q/DCC-ERG.

</details>


### [77] [From Isolated Tasks to Structured Capabilities: A Multilayer Taxonomy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22182)
*Shixin Fang,Jiachen Wo,Wenjuan Qin,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向大语言模型评测研究的能力分类体系，将任务层面的零散证据重组为能力层面的结构化图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测研究覆盖大量任务与基准，但证据主要按任务组织，导致跨研究比较困难、任务实际考察的能力不清晰，也难以系统发现评测覆盖空白。

Method: 作者构建了包含3个层级、14个能力域和91个子技能的多层分类法，能力定义参考人类认知科学而非LLM架构；并筛选2023—2025年ACL、AAAI、ICML、NeurIPS的31,505篇论文，对其中15,934篇LLM相关论文进行多模型标注、共识整合与仲裁映射。

Result: 研究关注高度集中于语言-语义能力、推理、规划与决策、感知等少数领域；6个能力域在论文中占比低于2%。多数领域内部存在强烈的子技能集中现象。语言-语义能力与推理是最高频组合，而心理理论与社会推理/交互在满足共现阈值的组合中提升度最高。

Conclusion: 该分类体系将LLM评测的分析单位从孤立任务转向结构化能力，有助于组织研究、审计覆盖范围、解释评测结果，并为诊断、训练和迁移研究提出可检验假设。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluation spans diverse tasks and benchmarks, yet evidence remains organized around tasks rather than the capabilities they probe. This fragmentation limits cross-study comparison, obscures capabilities tasks recruit, and makes coverage gaps difficult to identify.
  We introduce a multi-layer taxonomy of 14 capability domains and 91 subskills across Primitive, Constructed, and Integrative layers. Human cognitive science guides capability definition and organization, not LLM architecture. Layer assignments draw on developmental precedence and hypothesized functional support, while human-origin constructs are adapted to observable model behavior.
  To demonstrate operational utility, we screened 31,505 papers from ACL, AAAI, ICML, and NeurIPS between 2023 and 2025 and mapped 15,934 LLM-focused papers through multi-model annotation, consensus, and arbitration. Direct research attention concentrated on Language-Semantic Competence (3,551; 22.3%), Reasoning (3,388; 21.3%), Planning and Decision-Making (2,149; 13.5%), and Perception (1,954; 12.3%), whereas six domains appeared in fewer than 2% of papers. Within domains, the most frequent subskill had a median prevalence of 97.9% and appeared in at least 90% of papers in 10 of 14 domains. Language-Semantic Competence and Reasoning formed the highest-volume pair (n = 1,864; 11.7%; lift = 2.47), whereas Theory of Mind and Social Reasoning and Interaction showed the highest lift among pairs with at least 20 co-occurrences (n = 62; lift = 30.84).
  By shifting the unit of analysis from isolated tasks to structured capabilities, the taxonomy supports research organization, coverage audits, evaluation interpretation, and testable hypotheses for diagnosis, training, and transfer.

</details>


### [78] [Why Large Language Models and Humans Converge and Diverge in Evaluating Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22218)
*Pengzhao Lyu,Yeun Joon Kim,Hanlin Xiao,Yingyue Luna Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型作为创造力评估者时与人类评价的一致性及差异来源，发现LLM更依赖较窄的评价标准，尤其擅长与人类在新颖性维度对齐，但在需要社会、市场和声誉等情境信息的评价中明显偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被越来越多地用于创造力评价，其判断是否与人类一致的证据并不稳定。本文旨在解释LLM何时、为何会与人类创造力判断趋同或分歧，并识别不同模型背后的评价标准。

Method: 作者开展三项研究，覆盖六种常用LLM。研究1识别LLM使用的创造力评价标准并与人类标准比较；研究2基于1,103个创意检验LLM评分与人类评分的相关性及不同模型区分高低创造力创意的能力；研究3基于1,195个样本考察情境信息对人类与LLM创造力评分的影响。

Result: 研究1发现LLM通常只使用人类创造力评价标准中的较窄子集，与人类在新颖性维度最一致，在情境维度差异最大，且不同LLM标准广度差异明显。研究2显示LLM评价与人类评价中度相关，标准更广的LLM更能区分人类认为更有或更低创造力的想法。研究3发现情境信息会显著改变人类创造力评分，但对LLM评分影响很小。

Conclusion: LLM与人类创造力评价的一致性取决于评价所需证据类型及模型采用的标准。LLM在强调内在属性如新颖性时更接近人类，但在需要情境信息时容易偏离。因此，选择何种LLM作为创造力评估者会显著影响哪些创意被识别为有创造力。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of large language models (LLMs) as creativity evaluators, evidence of their alignment with human evaluations remains mixed, raising the question of when and why their judgments converge with or diverge from human judgments. Across three studies and six widely used LLMs, we addressed this gap by identifying the standards underlying LLM creativity evaluation and examining their downstream implications. Study 1 showed that LLMs generally relied on a narrower subset of human creativity evaluation standards. Convergence with human standards was strongest in the novelty dimension, whereas divergence was clearest in the contextual dimension, which captures social, market, and reputational information. Moreover, each LLM exhibited distinct, model-specific standards that varied substantially in breadth. These differences in evaluation standards were reflected in actual creativity judgments. Study 2 (N = 1,103 ideas) showed that LLM evaluations were moderately correlated with human evaluations, and individual LLMs with broader standards better distinguished ideas humans judged as more versus less creative. Study 3 (N = 1,195) showed that LLMs were less sensitive to contextual information: such information significantly altered human creativity ratings but left LLM ratings largely unchanged. Together, our findings help explain the mixed evidence on LLM-human alignment, showing that alignment depends on the evidence a judgment demands and the standards each model applies. LLMs may resemble humans when evaluations emphasize intrinsic qualities such as novelty, yet diverge when judgments require contextual information. Selecting an LLM evaluator is therefore a consequential decision: different models, applying different standards, recognize different ideas as creative.

</details>


### [79] [Biomedical Machine Translation for Low-Resource Arabic-Script Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22300)
*Abdullah Alabdullah,Arash Eslamighayour,Sarp Harbalioglu,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究医疗领域中面向阿拉伯文字语言的跨语言神经机器翻译迁移，利用阿拉伯语和波斯语作为高资源枢轴语言，提升达里语、普什图语、索拉尼库尔德语和乌尔都语等低资源目标语言的翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗/生物医学领域的低资源语言缺乏足够的NMT训练数据，尤其是使用阿拉伯文字的相关语言；在高风险医疗场景中，提升低资源语言翻译能力具有实际需求。

Method: 作者使用小型decoder-only LLM并进行LoRA微调，训练领域特定的枢轴语言适配器；比较三种迁移策略：few-shot上下文学习、少量监督适配，以及零目标数据的LoRA适配器合并。

Result: 仅用500句监督数据即可使达里语接近枢轴语言质量（CHrF++ 41.01），乌尔都语也获得明显提升（28.88）；达里语的零数据适配器合并结果距离监督适配仅差约3.5 CHrF++。普什图语和索拉尼库尔德语表现仍不足以支持高风险临床部署。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移在与枢轴语言亲缘或结构接近的低资源语言上效果显著，LoRA适配器合并即使没有目标语言生物医学数据也能有效工作；但当目标语言与枢轴语言结构距离较远时，迁移能力存在明显上限。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of healthcare-domain cross-lingual transfer to address the scarcity of biomedical NMT resources for Arabic-script languages. We use Arabic and Persian as higher-resource pivots to improve translation for \textbf{four severely low-resource} targets: Dari (Afghan Persian, a standardised variety of Persian), Pashto, Sorani Kurdish (Central Kurdish, a major standardized variety of Kurdish), and Urdu (closely related to Hindi). Using LoRA fine-tuning on small decoder-only LLMs, we train \textit{domain-specific pivot adapters} and evaluate \textbf{three transfer strategies}: few-shot in-context learning, minimal supervised adaptation, and, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time in this setting, zero-data LoRA adapter merging. Supervised adaptation with just 500 sentences achieves near pivot-language quality for Dari (CHrF++ 41.01) and meaningful gains for Urdu (28.88), while adapter merging reaches within 3.5 CHrF++ of supervised adaptation for Dari at zero additional cost. Pashto and Sorani Kurdish remain insufficient for high-stakes clinical deployment exposing the limits of cross-lingual transfer when structural distance from the pivots is too great. LoRA adapter merging works surprisingly well for closely related languages, even without target-language biomedical data.

</details>


### [80] [A Factorial Study of Synthetic Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation using Grammar Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22376)
*Varun Ghat Ravikumar,Sina Ahmadi,Lena Jäger,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用大语言模型从濒危/低资源语言的语法书中抽取规则、例句和词汇，生成合成平行语料用于机器翻译微调，在多个语言上取得优于仅用种子数据的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数濒危和极低资源语言缺少机器翻译所需的平行语料，但往往存在描述性语法书。既有方法多在推理时把语法内容放入提示词，难以系统转化为可训练资源。作者希望将静态语言学文献转化为可用于模型微调的合成数据。

Method: 构建一条流水线：使用大语言模型从语法书中抽取语法规则、示例句和词典信息，再生成合成平行语料；随后用这些合成数据对机器翻译模型进行微调。实验覆盖 Kalamang、Tuatschin、Mandan 三种类型差异较大的低资源语言，并通过 96 个配置的因子实验考察目标词性、检索粒度和样本量等因素。

Result: 相较种子数据基线，合成数据微调在 Kalamang 的 75% 配置、Tuatschin 的 59% 配置中带来提升；最佳 ChrF++ 增益分别达到 +8.8、+5.3、+3.3。因子实验揭示了哪些词性、检索粒度与样本量组合更有效，以及在何种条件下效果下降。

Conclusion: 静态语法文献可以被有效转化为机器翻译微调数据，为严重低资源语言构建翻译工具提供了可行路径；但收益依赖数据生成配置和语言条件，需要系统调参与失效分析。

Abstract: Most endangered languages lack the parallel data required for machine translation, despite the existence of descriptive grammar books. We introduce a pipeline that uses large language models to extract grammatical rules, example sentences, and lexicons from grammar books and generate synthetic parallel corpora for fine-tuning-rather than feeding grammar content into prompts at inference time, as in prior work. Validated on three typologically diverse low-resource languages-Kalamang (Papuan), Tuatschin (Romance), and Mandan (Siouan)-we show that fine-tuning on synthetic data improves over seed-data baselines in 75% of configurations for Kalamang and 59% for Tuatschin, with best-case ChrF++ gains of +8.8, +5.3, and +3.3 respectively. Through a systematic factorial study across 96 configurations varying target part-of-speech, retrieval granularity, and sample volume, we identify which factor combinations drive gains and where they break down. Our results demonstrate that static linguistic documentation can be repurposed for machine translation fine-tuning, offering a practical path towards translation tools for severely under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [81] [grapheme-kit: Grapheme-Level Metrics and Text Processing for Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22456)
*Izzath Nisfer,Ashini Kavindya,Ovindu Atukorala,Purushoth Velayuthan,Menan Velayuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 grapheme-kit，一个开源 Python 库，将常见词汇距离、相似度与评估指标从 Unicode 码点层面扩展到字素簇层面，以更准确评估复杂书写系统中的文本错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本距离、相似度和评估指标通常基于 Unicode code points 计算，但在某些书写系统中，一个视觉/语言意义上的字素可能由多个码点组成，因此码点级计算会误判或放大错误，尤其影响复杂文字系统的 OCR 等任务评估。

Method: 作者开发 grapheme-kit 库，使相关指标在 grapheme clusters 上运行；同时针对 Tamil 和 Sinhala 提供改进的字素处理能力，包括准确的字素簇识别以及字素组合/分解工具。并通过 OCR 案例研究比较字素级指标与传统码点级指标的效果。

Result: OCR 案例表明，字素级指标相较码点级指标能更忠实地反映复杂文字系统中的实际识别错误，避免因多码点字素导致的评估偏差。

Conclusion: grapheme-kit 为复杂书写系统提供了更合理的文本处理与评估基础，强调在涉及多码点字素的语言中，字素簇级别的度量比 Unicode 码点级别的度量更可靠。

Abstract: Existing lexical distance, similarity, and evaluation metrics operate on Unicode code points, which can misrepresent errors in writing systems where a single grapheme is represented by multiple Unicode code points. We introduce grapheme-kit, an open-source Python library that extends these metrics to operate on grapheme clusters instead. The library also provides improved grapheme processing for Tamil and Sinhala, including accurate grapheme cluster identification and grapheme composition/decomposition utilities. Through an OCR case study, we demonstrate that grapheme-level metrics provide a more faithful evaluation of complex scripts.

</details>


### [82] [Skill Self-Play: Pushing the Frontier of LLM Capability with Co-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22529)
*Siyuan Huang,Pengyu Cheng,Haotian Liu,Tao Chen,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Shijie Zhou,Ziyi Yang,Gangwei Jiang,Mengyu Zhou,Yu Cheng,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Skill Self-Play（Skill-SP），通过“技能”作为可验证执行与开放式任务多样性之间的中间层，让提议器、求解器和动态技能控制器在强化学习自博弈循环中共同进化，从而提升大语言模型在工具使用与推理任务上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 自进化方法存在任务多样性与验证可靠性之间的矛盾：依赖环境的方法反馈可靠但领域狭窄；开放式自生成方法任务空间更广但缺乏可靠验证，容易引入错误奖励并污染训练过程。论文希望找到一种既能保持可靠反馈、又能扩展任务空间的训练机制。

Method: 作者引入“agent skills”作为折中机制：每个技能对应特定场景中的深度、可验证执行，而在多个技能之间动态路由可维持开放式任务多样性。基于此提出 Skill-SP 框架，包含 proposer、solver 和 dynamic skill controller。proposer 根据动态采样技能生成挑战任务；solver 探索候选解以扩展能力边界；skill controller 收集执行反馈并更新、扩展技能库。三者通过强化学习式自博弈循环持续协同演化。

Result: 在工具使用和推理基准上的实验表明，Skill-SP 能作为稳健的模型进化引擎，持续提升已有较强 backbone 的性能上限，并显著改善初始对齐较差模型的表现。

Conclusion: Skill-SP 通过技能驱动的自博弈，将结构化可验证反馈与开放式探索结合起来，为 LLM 交互式自进化提供了一种有效路径。

Abstract: LLM training is shifting from manual design and annotation to interaction-driven self-evolution. However, existing self-evolutionary methods face a fundamental dilemma between task diversity and verification reliability: environment-bound methods obtain precise feedback but confine learning to narrow domains, while open-ended self-generation broadens the task space but lacks reliable verification, allowing misleading rewards to pollute the training loop. We identify agent skills as a powerful middle ground to reconcile this tension: each skill ensures deep, verifiable execution in a specific scenario, while dynamic routing across skills maintains open-ended task variety. Leveraging this insight, we introduce Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP), a co-evolutionary framework comprising a proposer, a solver, and a dynamic skill controller. Orchestrated via a reinforcement learning loop, these components co-evolve in a continuous self-play loop: the proposer generates challenging tasks conditioned on dynamically sampled skills; the solver explores candidate solutions to push its capability boundaries; and the skill controller collects execution feedback to update and expand the skill library. This interactive co-evolution effectively bridges the gap between structured verification and open-ended exploration. Empirical evaluations on tool-use and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Skill-SP, serving as a robust evolution engine, consistently pushes the performance ceiling of competent backbones while catalyzing striking turnarounds for initially misaligned models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/skill-self-play.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [FlowEvo: Self-Evolving Agents through the Co-Evolution of Workflows and Executable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21596)
*Zeyu Ren,Ling Yue,Ran Li,Yishu Wang,Shengxiang Xu,Hanmo Liu,Shaowu Pan,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEvo 是一个免训练框架，可将大语言模型智能体执行任务时的成功轨迹编译为可复用技能，并在后续推理中检索、执行与维护这些技能，从而提升准确率与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体能在推理阶段组合推理、工具调用和代码执行来解决复杂任务，但执行过程中发现的有效流程通常只服务于当前任务，缺乏系统化保存与复用机制，难以持续提升后续任务表现。

Method: 论文提出 FlowEvo，包含三个耦合机制：1）workflow-to-skill compilation：从成功执行轨迹中提取可复用的可调用工件，并配套结构化指导；2）skill-to-workflow feedback：在后续任务中从技能库检索技能，通过直接执行或上下文注入辅助求解；3）skill curation：监控技能的下游效用，抑制导致负迁移的技能。技能录入时尽可能进行接口、回放与安全检查。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、HumanEval 和 GSM8K 等交互环境与代码/数学生成基准上，FlowEvo 在作者实现设置下取得最佳准确率—成本权衡。ALFWorld 上成功率达到 82.8%，比最强基线高 23.6 个百分点，且平均每轮 token 使用量不到最高效基线的一半。消融实验显示三个机制均对整体性能有贡献。

Conclusion: FlowEvo 通过“工作流—技能—工作流”的反馈循环，使 LLM 智能体无需更新模型参数即可在推理时持续积累、复用和优化任务求解能力，提升跨任务效率与表现。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by constructing inference-time workflows that combine reasoning, tool use, and code execution. While such workflows enable flexible problem solving, the useful procedures discovered during execution are often transient: they help solve the current task but are not retained in a form that can systematically benefit future tasks. We present FlowEvo, a training-free framework that compiles successful traces into reusable skill records. Each record pairs a callable artifact with auxiliary structured guidance, and admission applies interface, replay, and safety checks where feasible. These skill records persist in a skill bank at inference time. FlowEvo is organized around three coupled mechanisms: (1)~workflow-to-skill compilation, which extracts reusable executable artifacts from successful traces; (2)~skill-to-workflow feedback, which retrieves accumulated skills to support future problem solving through either direct execution or structured context injection; and (3)~skill curation, which monitors downstream utility and suppresses skills that cause negative transfer. Through this workflow--skill--workflow feedback loop, FlowEvo enables agents to accumulate and refine task-solving capability over time without updating model parameters. Experiments on benchmarks spanning interactive environments (ALFWorld) and code/math generation (HumanEval, GSM8K) show that FlowEvo achieves the best accuracy-cost tradeoff among the evaluated baselines under our implementation settings. On ALFWorld, FlowEvo achieves an 82.8\% success rate, 23.6 percentage points above the strongest baseline, while its average token usage per episode is less than half that of the most efficient baseline. Controlled ablations confirm that each mechanism contributes to the overall result. The code is public at https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/FlowEvo.

</details>


### [84] [Risk Is Not the Target: A Monotonic Framework for Evaluating Wildfire Operational Risk Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21597)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Maxime Coulmeau,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出传统分类指标不适合评估连续型野火风险信号，提出单调性评估框架，并发现专家型 DFE 指数在风险等级与运营负载的一致性上表现最均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野火风险系统常用 F1-score、IoU 等机器学习指标评估，但这些指标关注事件预测准确性，无法衡量连续风险评分是否能与实际运营压力（火灾数量、干预时间、资源部署等）保持有序一致。

Method: 作者提出一种单调性评估框架，检验预测风险分数上升时，观测到的运营负载是否也稳定上升；并在法国 Alpes-Maritimes 地区比较三类系统：专家规则 DFE 指数、基于 GRU 的预测模型，以及结合预测 AI 与 LLM 推理的混合多智能体系统 FARS。

Result: 实验显示，DFE 虽然分类指标较差，但在完整风险尺度上具有最均衡的单调行为；GRU 模型局部单调性较强，但风险等级分布不均；FARS 未能修正上游信号的结构性局限，反而继承并暴露这些问题。

Conclusion: 论文主张评估野火风险模型应从事件预测准确性转向风险等级对运营动态的解释能力：好的风险模型不是单纯准确预测火灾，而是其有序风险尺度能稳定反映实际运营负载。

Abstract: Evaluating wildfire risk systems using standard machine-learning metrics such as F1-score or IoU is fundamentally flawed: these metrics assess event prediction accuracy, not the operational coherence of a continuous risk signal. This work proposes a novel monotonic evaluation framework that measures whether increases in a predicted risk score consistently correspond to increases in observed operational load, such as number of fires, intervention time, and deployed resources. Moreover, we compare three structurally different approaches on the French Alpes-Maritimes department: the expert-based DFE index, GRU- based predictive models, and FARS, a hybrid multi-agent system combining predictive AI with LLM-based reasoning. Experimental results reveal that the DFE, despite poor classification metrics, exhibits the most balanced monotonic behavior across the full risk scale. GRU models achieve strong local monotonicity but fail to produce well-distributed risk levels. FARS inherits and reveals the structural limitations of upstream signals rather than correcting them. The central finding is a paradigm shift: a good risk model does not predict fires accurately, but one whose ordinal scale meaningfully explains operational dynamics, as proved in this paper. Code of the monotonic framework is available on github.

</details>


### [85] [Securing Multimodal AI through Internal Information Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21600)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Hyeonjeong Ha,Qiusi Zhan,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowGuard 通过监测多模态模型内部的跨模态一致性，在推理时高效检测恶意/对抗输入，显著降低攻击成功率并保持较低效用损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在单模态系统没有的攻击面：攻击者可将恶意意图分散到文本与视觉等不同模态中，从而绕过仅检查单一模态的防护。因此，需要利用跨模态一致性作为检测信号，而不是孤立地审查各模态输入或输出。

Method: 提出 FlowGuard，一个轻量级推理时防御框架。其核心是监测模型内部多模态融合过程中的一致性，并构造受部分信息分解启发的 FlowVectors，用于量化跨模态冗余、协同信息以及模态特异性主导关系。方法以仅包含良性样本的数据训练一类分类器，判断融合后的多模态预测是否与单模态语义证据保持一致。

Result: 在未见过的攻击上，FlowGuard 将攻击成功率从超过 90% 降至低于 15%，效用损失小于 3%，并实现最高 6 倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 跨模态一致性是检测多模态对抗/有害输入的有效内部信号；相比依赖原始输入、输出或标量置信度的防御，FlowGuard 更高效、鲁棒且适合推理时部署。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models introduce attack surfaces absent in unimodal systems: adversaries can distribute malicious intent across modalities to evade unimodal safeguards. This motivates using cross-modal consistency as a detection signal rather than inspecting each modality in isolation. Our key observation is that benign inputs induce compatible predictive behavior from text-only and vision-only reasoning that stabilizes when fused, whereas adversarial manipulation disrupts this consistency, causing abnormal multimodal behavior. Existing defenses that examine raw inputs or outputs overlook this internal fusion process, rendering them brittle and computationally expensive. We propose FlowGuard, a lightweight inference-time framework that detects harmful inputs by monitoring internal multimodal consistency. Unlike approaches that rely on scalar confidence metrics, FlowGuard derives FlowVectors inspired by Partial Information Decomposition that quantify cross-modal redundancy, synergy, and modality-specific dominance, capturing whether fused multimodal predictions remain aligned with unimodal semantic evidence. In a one-class classification problem trained solely on benign data, FlowGuard reduces Attack Success Rates from >90% to <15% on unseen attacks, with <3% utility loss and up to a 6 times latency reduction. Our results demonstrate that monitoring cross-modal consistency offers an efficient and effective defense for multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [From Frame-Level Recognition to Event-Level Confirmation: Repair Traces and Runtime Failure Analysis of Public-Space Gesture Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21601)
*M. Meng,Yansong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“识别到交互鸿沟”问题，基于景区交互 kiosk 的工程维修记录归纳部署失败类型，并提出事件级运行时抽象以提升交互事件稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共空间手势交互常被按帧级识别准确性评估，但实际部署中用户感知的是意图动作能否转化为稳定、可恢复的交互事件。现有评估边界与部署失败边界不一致，因此需要从真实工程问题中分析失败机制。

Method: 作者分析某景区交互 kiosk 项目的 8 条工程维修记录，覆盖双手作揖、单手握拳摇动、双手抓取控制、知识图谱节点悬停 4 类手势任务；从中抽取 20 个失败实例，并归纳为 6 类非互斥工作失败类别，同时总结修复机制，构建位于手部关键点模型与交互任务之间的事件级运行时抽象。

Result: 论文得到一个部署场景驱动的失败分类体系，包括模型输出退化、时间错配、几何尺度不稳定、坐标渲染错配、运行时生命周期故障、反馈同步与恢复故障；并提出事件确认式运行时抽象，用于组织确认、稳定、恢复等交互层机制。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于以真实部署维修记录揭示帧级识别与事件级交互之间的差距，提供有限但实用的失败分类、运行时抽象和案例研究发现；其边界明确，不声称提出新识别模型、大规模用户评估或量化准确率提升。

Abstract: Public-space gesture interaction is often evaluated as a frame-level recognition problem, but deployed systems expose a different failure boundary. In scenic kiosks, exhibition halls, and service terminals, users experience whether an intended action becomes a stable interaction event, not whether individual hand-landmark frames are correct. We call this the recognition-to-interaction gap.
  This paper analyzes 8 engineering repair records from a scenic-area interactive kiosk project, covering 4 gesture tasks: two-hand bowing, single-hand fist shaking, two-hand catching control, and knowledge-graph node hovering. From these traces, we extract 20 failure instances and organize them into six non-exclusive working failure classes: model-output degeneration, temporal mismatch, geometric-scale instability, coordinate-rendering mismatch, runtime lifecycle failure, and feedback synchronization and recovery failure.
  We further organize recurring repair mechanisms into an event-level runtime abstraction between the hand-landmark model and the interaction task. The contribution is deliberately bounded: a deployment-grounded failure taxonomy, an event-confirmation runtime abstraction, and case-study findings. We do not claim a new recognition model, large-scale user evaluation, or quantified accuracy gains.

</details>


### [87] [Transferable Latency Prediction for Fast LLM Screening on Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21602)
*Xiaolong Tu,Vinod K. Mishra,Venkat R. Dasari,Anu G. Bourgeois,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向部署的、运行时感知的LLM边缘设备延迟预测框架，通过融合静态配置与动态硬件遥测并进行轻量校准，提高跨设备延迟预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构边缘设备上部署大语言模型时，推理延迟受模型结构、prompt行为、运行时后端、硬件利用率、DVFS与热状态等多因素影响；传统仅依赖静态特征的预测难以准确适配不同设备和运行环境，导致模型选择与部署成本高。

Method: 将每个推理请求表示为硬件-运行时-模型-prompt配置，将推理过程拆分为prefill与decode两个阶段；使用门控预测模型自适应融合静态描述符和动态硬件遥测信息，并通过轻量校准支持跨设备迁移。

Result: 在Pixel 8上，相比静态基线，总延迟R²由0.953提升至0.960，decode延迟R²由0.957提升至0.973；在Pixel 8 Pro上，prefill延迟R²由-1.383提升至0.966。跨设备迁移中，经校准后Pixel 8 Pro到Pixel 8的总延迟R²由-0.974提升至0.940，decode延迟R²由-1.085提升至0.927。异构测试显示同一SmolLM2模型在Orange Pi 5 Pro为8.42 tokens/s，在RTX 3090级GPU为64.38 tokens/s。

Conclusion: 运行时感知的延迟预测结合轻量校准能够显著提升边缘异构平台上的LLM延迟估计能力，降低profiling成本，并支持更可靠的延迟感知模型选择与部署。

Abstract: Accurate latency prediction is critical for deploying large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous edge devices, where inference latency is affected by model architecture, prompt behavior, runtime backend, hardware utilization, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS), and thermal variation. This paper presents a runtime-aware latency prediction framework for deployment-oriented LLM selection. The framework represents each inference request as a hardware-runtime-model-prompt configuration, separates inference into prefill and decode phases, and adaptively fuses static descriptors with dynamic hardware telemetry through a gated prediction model.
  We evaluate the framework using Pixel mobile devices and validate the profiling pipeline on Jetson Nano, Orange Pi 5 Pro, and an RTX 3090-class GPU platform. On Pixel 8, the full predictor improves total-latency R-squared from 0.953 to 0.960 and decode-latency R-squared from 0.957 to 0.973 over a static-only baseline. On Pixel 8 Pro, it improves prefill-latency R-squared from -1.383 to 0.966. For cross-device transfer, calibration improves Pixel 8 Pro to Pixel 8 total-latency R-squared from -0.974 to 0.940 and decode-latency R-squared from -1.085 to 0.927. Heterogeneous profiling further shows that latency is highly device- and runtime-dependent: the same SmolLM2 model family reaches 8.42 tokens/s on Orange Pi 5 Pro but 64.38 tokens/s on an RTX 3090-class GPU. These results demonstrate that runtime-aware prediction with lightweight calibration can reduce profiling cost and support latency-aware LLM deployment across heterogeneous edge platforms.

</details>


### [88] [AgentKVShift: Efficient KV Cache Reuse for Agentic Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21604)
*Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Jason Kong,Lanxiang Hu,Quanling Zhao,Yujie Zhao,Onat Gungor,Hao Zhang,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentKVShift 是一种面向记忆增强 LLM 智能体的免训练 KV 缓存复用与校正方法，通过少量 probe 估计每个记忆单元的共享残差偏移，在仅刷新 10–30% 缓存的情况下接近全量重计算效果，并显著降低预填充延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强 LLM 智能体需要跨大量交互维护上下文，其结构化记忆单元在每次检索后都需重新编码为 KV states，导致 prefill latency 高。现有免训练 KV 复用方法主要针对 RAG 原始文本片段，在包含摘要、关键词、标签等元数据的结构化 agentic memories 上效果下降。

Method: 论文提出 AgentKVShift：一种 training-free、probe-guided 的 KV residual correction 方法，按检索到的 memory unit 运行。核心观察是每个 memory 的 KV 复用残差可分解为共享的 memory-level offset 与较小的 token-wise fluctuation。方法利用小规模 probe set 估计该 offset，并对所有复用 token 施加单一加权校正；同时对部分 token 进行重计算，将刷新预算转化为对整个 chunk 的有效校正信号。

Result: 在 4 个 3B 到 32B 参数规模的开源 LLM，以及两个长程 agentic memory benchmark（长期对话与智能体应用）上，AgentKVShift 在仅刷新 10–30% KV cache 时达到接近全量重计算的性能，优于相同重计算比例下的基线方法。相比先前方法需要 45–55% refresh 才能达到类似性能，AgentKVShift 最多可降低 5 倍重计算量，并在单张 A100 上相较无 KV 复用获得 2–3.5 倍 prefill 加速。与 KV cache quantization 结合时，在 2-bit 和 4-bit 激进量化下仍保留超过先前复用方法 2 倍的 F1。

Conclusion: AgentKVShift 表明结构化 agentic memory 的 KV 复用误差具有可利用的 memory-level 残差结构。通过少量 probe 进行全局残差校正，可在低刷新比例下兼顾性能与推理效率，并能与 KV 量化正交结合，适合长程记忆增强智能体的低延迟部署。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents maintain context across hundreds of interactions through agentic memory systems that actively curate retrieved content with LLM-generated metadata such as summaries, keywords, and tags. From an inference cost standpoint, every retrieval triggers a full re-encoding of these structured memory units into Key-Value (KV) states, which dominates prefill latency. Existing training-free KV reuse methods mitigate this by selectively recomputing a small fraction of tokens, but were designed for RAG-style raw passages and degrade on structured agentic memories. We present AgentKVShift, a training-free, probe-guided KV residual correction method that operates per retrieved memory unit. One of the crucial insights we demonstrate is that the per-memory KV reuse residual decomposes into a shared memory-level offset plus small token-wise fluctuations. Estimating this offset from a small probe set allows us to correct every reused token by a single weighted correction. Unlike prior reuse methods which decide which tokens to recompute and leave the rest of the cache stale, AgentKVShift also corrects the tokens it does not recompute, turning the refresh budget into useful signal across the entire chunk. Across four open-source LLMs spanning 3B to 32B parameters and two long-horizon agentic memory benchmarks (long-term dialogue and agentic applications), AgentKVShift achieves near full recompute performance while refreshing only 10-30% of the cache, outperforming baselines at the same recompute ratio. It requires up to 5x lower recompute to reach this near-full performance, which prior reuse methods only attain at 45-55% refresh. In this regime, AgentKVShift delivers prefill speedups of 2-3.5x over no-KV-reuse on a single A100. AgentKVShift orthogonally composes with KV cache quantization, retaining over 2x the F1 of prior reuse methods under aggressive 2- and 4-bit settings.

</details>


### [89] [TILT: Improving Compositional Generation in Diffusion Models with a Model-Intrinsic Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21606)
*Debottam Dutta,Jaehoon Hahm,Jianchong Chen,Romit Roy Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TILT 是一种无需训练、无需外部奖励模型的测试时引导框架，通过基于基础扩散模型自身分布构造奖励，提升复杂组合提示词下的文本到图像生成一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强大的文本到图像模型在复杂组合提示中仍会出现概念遗漏、属性绑定错误或多概念混淆等组合失败；因此需要一种测试时方法，在不重新训练模型的情况下调整采样轨迹，使生成图像更忠实于提示中的多个概念。

Method: 作者将组合失败解释为联合概念分布与单概念分布之间的重叠模式，并定义一种内在奖励以偏好所有概念同时出现的样本。该奖励不依赖外部监督或奖励模型，进一步形成 KL 约束优化目标，推导出闭式 tilted target distribution，并据此设计扩散采样中的指导步骤。基于概念分布交互，提出两类引导策略，并通过混合策略平衡二者优势。

Result: 在 T2ICompBench 提示集上的实验表明，TILT 相比既有基线提升了组合对齐能力，同时保持了较好的图像质量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用基础模型自身的分布信息进行测试时奖励对齐，可以有效缓解文本到图像生成中的组合性问题，是一种训练自由、原则化且实用的采样改进方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in powerful text-to-image generation models have made it increasingly important to develop test-time methods that modify the sampling trajectory to produce images more faithful to complex compositional prompts. We present TILT, a training-free framework for compositional text-to-image generation via test-time reward alignment. We interpret compositional failures as overlap modes between joint and single-concept distributions, and define a reward that favors samples where all concepts are jointly present. This reward is intrinsic to the base model and does not require any external supervision or reward models. This yields a KL-constrained objective with a closed-form tilted target distribution and principled guiding steps for diffusion sampling. The interaction of concept distributions together with the above reward naturally leads to two different guidance strategies while a hybrid approach that balances their respective benefits produces stronger performance. Experiments on prompts from T2ICompBench show that our method improves compositional alignment while preserving image quality compared to previous baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Spectral Flow Certificates for Depth-Aware Long-Range Propagation in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21607)
*Ranjan Veerabhadraswamy,Ajith Jubilson Emerson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spectral Flow Certificates（SFCs），一种无需训练和标签、仅由归一化拉普拉斯谱计算的单标量指标，用于在训练前预测图拓扑是否支持 GNN 完成长程信息传播任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN 依赖局部消息传递，某些图拓扑会限制远距离节点间的信息流动，导致长程任务即使训练充分也难以解决。现有方法缺乏一种廉价、训练前、无标签的诊断手段来判断新图结构是否适合给定深度的 GNN。

Method: 作者基于图的归一化拉普拉斯矩阵构造 SFC，将代数连通度与消息传递层数/深度预算融合为一个标量，用以衡量在给定层数下可穿越关键谱瓶颈的程度。并将其与谱间隙、平均有效电阻、图直径等结构统计量进行对比。

Result: 在 25 类合成图族和 150 个真实分子图拓扑上，SFC 能在训练前预测 GNN 长程任务准确率；在所有测试深度下解释力超过 90%，且相比平均有效电阻和图直径等经典指标能解释两倍以上的准确率方差。

Conclusion: 单次特征值计算即可在昂贵训练前识别受拓扑限制的图，SFC 可作为 GNN 部署中的原则性预筛选工具，尤其适用于长程依赖任务和不同模型深度设置。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks propagate information through local message passing, but the graph topologies themselves can silently prevent any amount of training from solving long-range tasks. When we deploy GNNs on new graphs, there is currently no inexpensive way to know, before training begins, whether the graphs' structures will allow information to travel far enough between distant nodes. We address this gap by proposing Spectral Flow Certificates (SFCs), single scalars computed from the graphs' normalised Laplacians in seconds, requiring no model training and no labelled data. An SFC fuses a graph's algebraic connectivity with the chosen message-passing depth into one number that measures how much of the critical spectral bottleneck can be traversed within the available depth budget. Unlike raw spectral gaps, which are static and depth-agnostic, SFCs adapt as the number of layers increases and therefore carry strictly more diagnostic information when depths vary. Compared with classical structural statistics such as average effective resistance and graph diameter, SFCs explain more than twice as much variance in trained GNN long-range accuracy. Across twenty-five synthetic graph families spanning paths, cycles, grids, regular graphs, and random graphs, SFCs predict trained accuracy before any gradients are computed, achieving explanatory power above ninety percent at all tested depths. The same predictive relationships hold on one hundred fifty real molecular graph topologies drawn from three independent benchmark datasets, confirming that the findings are not artefacts of their synthetic construction. Taken together, these results show that a single eigenvalue computation is sufficient to flag topology-limited graphs before committing to expensive training pipelines, providing a principled first filter for GNN deployments.

</details>


### [91] [Coupled Hierarchical Search over Topology and Execution for Agentic Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21609)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HierFlow 是一种无需训练、在测试时自动设计智能体工作流的分层搜索框架，通过拓扑调整与子工作流搜索协同提升复杂任务求解效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工作流可增强大语言模型处理复杂问题的能力，但自动生成工作流面临巨大的组合搜索空间，传统方法往往依赖离线训练，成本高且灵活性不足。

Method: 论文将工作流生成建模为拓扑搜索与执行搜索相互耦合的问题：高层拓扑决定子任务边界，低层执行结果反过来调整拓扑。提出 HierFlow，结合反馈引导的拓扑修改、受 MCTS 启发的快速树搜索来优化子工作流，并使用智能门控模块按需触发执行级搜索。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和代码生成基准上的实验显示，HierFlow 相比强基线持续取得更优表现，并在结果质量与计算效率之间实现较好平衡。

Conclusion: HierFlow 表明，通过测试时分层搜索与按需执行优化，可以在无额外训练开销的情况下自动生成高效、灵活的智能体工作流。

Abstract: Although structured workflows empower Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle complex problems, automating their creation is severely hindered by a vast combinatorial search space, frequently resulting in inflexible and resource-heavy offline training dependencies. To address this, we conceptualize workflow generation as an intertwined topology-and-execution search paradigm, where the broader topological layer dictates subtask boundaries and lower-level execution outcomes actively reshape the topology itself. Building on this foundation, we introduce HierFlow, a training-free, test-time hierarchical search architecture that automates agentic workflow design by merging feedback-guided topology adjustments with a fast, MCTS-inspired tree search for sub-workflow optimization. HierFlow maximizes efficiency through an intelligent gating module that selectively triggers execution-level searches based on contextual necessity, a mechanism we further support with an in-depth analysis detailing how varying degrees of cross-task coupling impact the effectiveness of hierarchical splitting. Comprehensive testing across question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks confirms that HierFlow consistently outperforms strong baselines, delivering an optimal balance of high-quality results and computational efficiency without any additional training overhead.

</details>


### [92] [SCOPE and SCION: A Benchmark and an Auditable Reference Pipeline for Schema Induction and Fusion from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21610)
*Miaobo Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Shuhao Hu,Bokun Wang,Rui Chen,Xin Wang,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOPE 基准与 SCION 参考流水线，用于从训练文本中归纳信息抽取所需的模式图，并在多种 schema-graph 指标上优于若干基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 schema-grounded 信息抽取与知识图谱构建通常默认已有 schema，但 schema 图本身是上游瓶颈；因此需要一个仅基于训练文本的 corpus-to-schema induction 评测基准和可审计参考方法。

Method: 构建 SCOPE：整合 24 个公开信息抽取来源（15 个关系抽取、9 个事件抽取），规范化为仅用于评测的 gold schema graphs。提出 SCION：从训练文本构造候选空间，并在严格 JSON 契约下进行命名、合并、过滤、验证和保守 schema fusion，所有操作限定在候选证据关联范围内。

Result: 在 SCOPE 核心套件中，SCION-lite 在 Literal、Fuzzy、Continuous、Graph 等 schema-graph 指标下，相比 released source-schema references、Text2Onto-style、LLM-only、matched extract-then-aggregate 等基线取得最高 F1；SCION-RL 以较小开放模型降低对专有 LLM 的依赖。

Conclusion: SCOPE 提供了系统化评测 corpus-to-schema induction 的基准，SCION 展示了可审计、证据关联的 schema 构建流程的有效性；结果应理解为针对规范化 typed-edge targets 的性能提升，而非声称自动归纳 schema 超越人工本体设计。

Abstract: Schema graphs are an upstream bottleneck of schema-grounded information extraction and knowledge graph construction, yet most extraction systems assume the schema is already available. We introduce SCOPE (Schema Construction and Ontology-induction Pipeline Evaluation), a train-text-only benchmark for corpus-to-schema induction and optional schema fusion from raw text, built from 24 public information extraction sources (15 RE and 9 EE) normalized into evaluation-only gold schema graphs; its core event-extraction target covers event types and within-event argument roles, with inter-event links reported separately. We present SCION (Schema Construction and Induction with Ontology Normalization), an auditable reference pipeline rather than a new extraction architecture; it constructs candidate spaces from train text and restricts naming, merging, filtering, validation, and conservative fusion to candidate-linked evidence under strict JSON contracts. On the SCOPE core suite, SCION-lite attains the highest F1 among released source-schema references, Text2Onto-style, LLM-only, and matched extract-then-aggregate baselines under Literal, Fuzzy, Continuous, and Graph schema-graph metrics, while the compact open-model SCION-RL variant reduces reliance on proprietary LLM schema engineers. These results are reported against normalized typed-edge targets rather than as claims that induced schemas surpass human ontology design; the release includes evidence-linked outputs, parse/fallback logs, candidate retention/merging logs, run manifests, code, and benchmark packages at https://github.com/wandugu/paper_scion.

</details>


### [93] [Procedural Knowledge Is Not Low-Rank: Why LoRA Fails to Internalize Multi-Step Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21612)
*Simon Dennis,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo,Rivaan Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，在需要多步流程与条件分支的程序性知识任务中，LoRA在保持参数效率的常用低秩范围内显著弱于全量微调，且提高rank也难以弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA等参数高效微调方法已广泛用于大语言模型适配，但其在指令跟随、风格迁移和事实适配之外，是否能有效学习复杂的程序性知识尚不明确。作者希望评估LoRA在面向智能体应用的多步骤、分支流程任务中的能力边界。

Method: 作者在程序化旅行预订任务上系统比较LoRA不同rank设置（r=16–128）与全量微调，并进一步在Zoom支持和保险理赔等跨领域流程任务上复现实验。同时，通过对全量微调产生的权重变化进行SVD分析，估计更新矩阵的有效秩以及低秩近似可捕获的Frobenius范数比例。

Result: 在旅行预订任务中，所有LoRA配置任务成功分数均显著低于全量微调（≤2.54 vs. 4.11，p<0.001），即使对话完成率仍达95–99%。在Zoom支持和保险理赔任务中，LoRA在r=32和r=128下平均仍落后全量微调0.8–2.2分，复杂流程差距最大。将rank从32提升到128仅带来有限改善。SVD结果显示，全量微调更新的平均有效秩约为761–1026，rank 128仅捕获43–51%的平方Frobenius范数。

Conclusion: 论文认为，程序性知识学习需要较高秩的参数更新，而LoRA的低秩约束难以表达这些变化。因此，在复杂流程执行和智能体场景中，LoRA存在根本性局限，不能简单替代全量微调。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like LoRA have become the default for adapting large language models, succeeding across instruction following, style transfer, and factual adaptation. We show that for procedural knowledge--the ability to follow multi-step procedures with conditional branching through to terminal states--LoRA fails to match full fine-tuning at the ranks where it retains its efficiency advantage. In a systematic ablation (r = 16--128) on a procedural travel booking task (14 nodes), all LoRA configurations fail uniformly (task success <= 2.54 vs. 4.11 for full fine-tuning, all p < 0.001), with scores decreasing at higher ranks--despite maintaining 95--99% conversation completion rates. Cross-domain replication on Zoom support (14 nodes) and insurance claims (55 nodes) at 8B confirms the failure generalizes: LoRA underperforms full fine-tuning by 0.8--2.2 points on average at both r = 32 and r = 128, with the largest gap on the most complex procedure. Quadrupling rank from 32 to 128 provides marginal improvement but does not close the gap. SVD analysis of the weight changes produced by full fine-tuning explains why: across three domains at both 3B and 8B, the mean effective rank of the update ranges from 761 to 1,026, and rank 128 captures only 43--51% of the squared Frobenius norm. Together, these findings establish that for procedural tasks LoRA falls well short of full fine-tuning--a fundamental limitation for agentic applications.

</details>


### [94] [The Hard Decision Layer: Evidence for Committed Inference in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21613)
*Ashwath Vaithinathan Aravindan,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了多选问答中 Transformer 语言模型的“硬决策层”（HDL）现象：答案选项排序会在推理中某一层突然稳定，且该层在不同模型、数据集和微调条件下具有一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望理解 Transformer 语言模型在多选问答推理过程中，究竟在网络的哪个阶段以及通过何种机制形成最终答案偏好，从而为模型可解释性、高效推理和模型控制提供依据。

Method: 作者在 Qwen、Llama、Granite、Mistral 四类模型和四个基准数据集上分析逐层答案选项排序的变化，识别排序突然稳定的 Hard Decision Layer；同时考察微调前后 HDL 的不变性，并通过标签格式与问题复杂度等系统消融实验验证该现象是否由架构而非外部因素驱动。

Result: 实验显示，不同模型和数据集上均稳定出现 HDL，且不依赖学习到的路由策略；HDL 对微调保持不变。在 HDL 处模型准确率出现显著跃升，例如 Qwen 在 CommonsenseQA 上最高提升 +0.61，随后性能趋于稳定。消融实验表明标签形式和问题复杂度变化并不能消除该现象。

Conclusion: HDL 可能是 Transformer 推理过程中的一种自然架构属性，揭示了模型在中间层完成答案承诺的机制。该发现有助于提升对语言模型推理动态的理解，并可能用于早停推理、计算效率优化和模型行为 steering。

Abstract: We investigate where and how transformer-based language models commit to predictions in multiple-choice question answering. We identify the _Hard Decision Layer_ (HDL), a natural architectural property where answer option rankings stabilize abruptly during inference. Empirical validation across four language models (Qwen, Llama, Granite, Mistral) and four benchmark datasets demonstrates consistent HDL emergence without learned routing policies. We also show that the HDL is invariant to fine-tuning. Our results reveal striking accuracy improvements at the HDL: up to +0.61 (Qwen on CommonsenseQA), after which performance stabilizes. Systematic ablations on label formats and problem complexity confirm the phenomenon is fundamental to model architecture. These findings offer mechanistic insights into transformer inference and suggest opportunities for efficient reasoning and model steering. All code and results required to reproduce this work are available in https://github.com/Mystic-Slice/hard-decision-layer

</details>


### [95] [Household Movement Detection in Mixed-Format Occupancy Data Using LLM-Based Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21614)
*Sasirekha Oguri,John R. Talburt,Mert Can Cakmak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个结合LLM实体抽取、语义嵌入相似度与图推理的实体解析框架，用于从非标准化姓名-地址数据中发现家庭共同迁移带来的间接实体链接，并在SPX基准上提升召回率与F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实体解析主要依赖记录两两相似度比较，难以捕捉人口居住数据中的间接关系，尤其是多个个体随家庭共同迁移所形成的群体级关联；同时，混合格式记录、噪声、重复数据以及缺乏稳定标识符增加了检测难度。

Method: 方法包括三部分：使用基于提示的大语言模型进行命名实体识别，从非标准化文本中提取个人姓名和地址；使用语义文本嵌入计算更鲁棒的相似度；构建图结构并通过图推理推断家庭共同迁移导致的群体级间接链接。

Result: 在由Synthetic Occupancy Generator生成的SPX基准数据集S8-S12上，相比强两两匹配基线，引入家庭迁移间接证据后召回率提升8-15%，同时保持较高精度，F1分数提升6-8%。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在实体解析中显式建模家庭共同迁移等间接关系，可以弥补传统两两相似度方法的不足，并在噪声较多、格式不统一的姓名-地址数据中提高链接发现能力。

Abstract: Entity resolution (ER) typically relies on pairwise similarity comparisons between records, which limits its ability to capture indirect relationships present in demographic occupancy data. An important indirect pattern arises from household movement, where multiple individuals relocate together across addresses, but detecting such patterns is difficult due to mixed-format records, noise, duplication, and the absence of stable identifiers. This paper proposes an AI-enhanced framework for detecting indirect entity links associated with household movement in unstandardized name-address data. The approach integrates prompt-based large language model (LLM) named entity recognition for extracting personal names and addresses without extensive preprocessing, semantic text embeddings for robust similarity computation, and graph-based reasoning to infer group-level movement patterns. Experimental evaluation on SPX benchmark datasets (S8-S12) generated using the Synthetic Occupancy Generator demonstrates that incorporating indirect household movement evidence improves recall by 8-15% while maintaining high precision, yielding F1-score gains of 6-8% over a strong pairwise baseline.

</details>


### [96] [FrED: External Data Influence Estimation via Domain Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21615)
*Theodoros Aivalis,Iraklis A. Klampanos,Antonis Troumpoukis,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种黑盒训练数据归因框架，将连续特征相似度与离散领域知识图谱融合，用于生成式 AI 输出的可解释归因与领域约束检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 快速部署使训练数据归因的透明性与问责性需求上升；现有参数化方法依赖模型权重、计算代价高，而传统相似度方法忽略深层结构和领域上下文。

Method: 构建概率框架，在完全黑盒条件下融合连续特征相似度与领域知识图谱，奖励具有高度特异性的历史样本，并抑制泛化背景样本对归因结果的主导。

Result: 在抽象艺术图像生成任务中，线性数据建模得分优于标准黑盒相似度基线，并缩小与基于梯度估计器的差距；在环境预测案例中，利用领域知识图谱检索物理一致的历史类比样本，相比仅基于潜空间的方法提升地理定位能力。

Conclusion: 该方法无需访问模型内部参数，能够提供高效、可解释、后验的影响分析与领域 grounded 检索机制，适用于结构上下文复杂的跨领域任务。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of generative AI has amplified the critical need for Training Data Attribution to ensure transparency and accountability. However, current parametric approaches require computationally prohibitive access to model weights, while similarity-based methods ignore deep structural context. We propose a novel probabilistic framework that operates entirely in a black-box setting. Our method fuses continuous feature similarities with discrete, domain-specific Knowledge Graphs (KGs). This approach ensures the attribution is grounded in structural reality, explicitly rewarding highly specific historical samples while preventing generic background data from dominating the results. We evaluate our framework across two distinct domains where linking outputs to data and domain context is inherently complex: abstract artistic image synthesis and high-dimensional physical weather forecasting. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates the robust efficacy of our approach. In the artistic domain, it achieves a strong Linear Datamodeling Score that exceeds standard black-box similarity baselines, while closing much of the gap to gradient-based estimators. We additionally present a cross-domain feasibility case study in environmental forecasting, where we use domain KGs to retrieve physically consistent historical analogs for regional flood forecasts, improving geographic localisation over a latent-only baseline. Operating entirely without internal model access, our approach provides an efficient, interpretable mechanism for post-hoc influence analysis and domain-grounded retrieval.

</details>


### [97] [Lost in Context: Addressing Context Anxiety in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21616)
*Ifueko Igbinedion,Jillian Ross,Etienne Ricardez,Sertac Karaman,Eric So*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推理模型在具备解题能力时仍因“上下文焦虑”而提前自我怀疑、放弃或低效推理的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，推理模型失败主要源于能力不足；但作者观察到，前沿推理模型有时具备解决问题所需能力，却会因为误判任务所需上下文或 token 预算而表现不佳。因此，论文旨在解释这种非能力性失败来源，并评估其对效率和性能的影响。

Method: 作者对“上下文焦虑”进行系统性分析，考察模型在长程任务中对所需 token 数的估计能力，以及在感知到上下文限制时的行为变化；同时比较模型是否能够学习替代性解题策略，以在长任务中避免过早自我怀疑。

Result: 研究发现，上下文焦虑部分来源于模型无法准确估计完成任务所需的 token 数；当模型认为自身受到上下文约束时，会产生实质性的效率损失。实验还表明，模型可以学习不引发上下文焦虑的长程问题求解策略。

Conclusion: 提升推理模型表现不一定只能依赖扩大模型能力或规模；改进模型对自身限制的评估能力、token 需求预测能力和自适应策略，可能同样带来显著性能提升。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom suggests that reasoning models fail when problems exceed their capabilities. However, we find that frontier reasoning models sometimes possess the necessary capabilities to solve problems but fail due to premature self-doubt -- a phenomenon informally known as context anxiety. We provide the first systematic study of context anxiety, demonstrating that it arises, in part, from a model's inability to accurately estimate the tokens required to complete a task. We also show that context anxiety leads to material efficiency losses when models operate under perceived constraints. Building on this analysis, we further show that models can learn alternative strategies for solving long-horizon problems without exhibiting context anxiety, suggesting that performance improvements may be achievable not through scaling model capabilities, but by improving models' ability to accurately assess and adapt to their own limitations.

</details>


### [98] [Do VLMs Read or Rewrite? On Transcription Faithfulness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21617)
*Gwang Gook Lee,Kenan Emir Ak,Jay Mohta,Yan Xu,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出VLM在文档理解中可能并非忠实转录器，而会将受扰动文本改写为更合理的形式；提出FaithC4多语扰动基准并系统评测15类系统，发现通用VLM更易发生该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统干净文本OCR基准难以发现VLM在面对不完美文本时的“合理化改写”行为。随着VLM替代传统OCR用于文档理解，评估其转录忠实性变得重要，尤其是在多语言与受扰动文档场景下。

Method: 构建FaithC4基准，包含英语、中文、韩文共1,455页单页文档，并设计三类扰动：字符打乱、随机替换、视觉相似替换。评估15个系统，覆盖通用VLM、OCR专用VLM和传统OCR管线；同时对Qwen3-VL-4B进行逐层探测，分析FFN表示与改写行为的关系。

Result: 在英语扰动下，通用VLM的WER最多恶化4.5个百分点，OCR专用VLM恶化0.2至2个百分点，传统OCR低于0.6个百分点。Qwen3-VL-4B中，当受扰动词最终层FFN表示仍接近原始编码时更易触发改写；当表示充分偏离时则更倾向忠实转录。短词4至6字符改写率最高可达10%，8字符以上几乎降为0。

Conclusion: VLM在文档转录中存在系统性的非忠实改写风险，尤其是通用VLM面对轻微扰动时可能输出语义上更合理但视觉上不准确的文本。FaithC4为检测此类问题提供了有效基准，结果表明OCR专用模型和传统OCR在转录忠实性上更稳定。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of traditional OCR pipelines for document understanding. In this paper, we show they do not always act as faithful transcribers: when text is imperfect, they often tend to rewrite it into a more plausible form - a behavior that clean-text OCR benchmarks cannot detect. We introduce FaithC4, a multilingual perturbation benchmark of 1,455 single-page documents (English, Chinese, Korean) with three perturbation families: scramble, random substitution, and visually similar substitution. We use the benchmark to evaluate 15 systems spanning general-purpose VLMs, OCR-specialized VLMs, and traditional OCR pipelines. These three categories differ in WER degradation under perturbation: general-purpose VLMs degrade by up to 4.5 points, OCR-specialized VLMs by 0.2-2 points, and traditional OCR by less than 0.6 points on English. Probing Qwen3-VL-4B layer-by-layer, we identify a consistent pattern: rewriting fires only when a perturbed word's final layer FFN representation stays close to the original encoding; when the representation diverges sufficiently, the model transcribes faithfully. Word length affects rewriting rate: short words (4-6 characters) are rewritten up to 10% of the time, with a sharp cutoff at 8 characters above which rewriting drops to 0%.

</details>


### [99] [LeafData: An Agentic System for Data Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21618)
*Sadanand Katukuri,Rajasekhar Bada,Navya Induri,Rohit Gandham,Lynette Pinto,Joses Selvan,Abishek Krishnamoorthy,Joseph Rozario,Pu Tian,Pavan Poudel,Yalong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeafData 是一个将用户意图转化为可验证、可执行 JSON 配置的数据迁移智能体系统，用于减少人工编写配置的复杂性与错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据迁移依赖 JSON 配置来定义数据连接、流水线逻辑和编排行为，但这要求用户具备较强领域知识，且手工配置耗时、易出错。

Method: 系统由前端聊天机器人和后端服务组成：聊天机器人逐步收集用户信息并进行基于 schema 的验证；后端处理已验证输入并生成可被编排平台直接消费的 JSON 配置产物。

Result: LeafData 能生成端到端数据迁移流水线所需的配置，使其无需人工编码即可执行，并支持关系型数据库、文件系统、文档数据库和 REST API 等异构数据源与连接器。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过智能体式交互、结构化信息收集和自动配置生成，可以降低数据迁移门槛，提高配置可靠性与流水线生成效率。

Abstract: Modern data migration relies on JSON configuration to define data connection, pipeline logic, and orchestration behavior. This requires domain knowledge from users and is time-consuming and error-prone. In this paper, we present LeafData, an agentic system that converts user intent into validated and executable JSON configuration for data migration. Specifically, LeafData comprises a frontend chatbot and the backend service. The chatbot incrementally collects required information from users and performs schema-driven validation, while the backend service processes validated inputs and generates JSON configuration artifacts. These artifacts are directly consumable by orchestration platforms, enabling end-to-end pipeline generation and execution without manual coding. LeafData supports heterogeneous data migration across various data sources and connectors including relational databases, file-based systems, document-oriented databases, and REST APIs.

</details>


### [100] [From Profiles to Steering Vectors: Global Sparse Priors and Local Semantic Calibration for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21620)
*Liuji Chen,Zeyu Zhang,Xinyuan Zhang,Shuai Nie,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLASS 是一种无需训练的个性化文本生成框架，通过稀疏自编码器提取全局用户风格先验，并结合局部对比式风格向量，在推理时进行分层激活引导，实现更稳健的风格个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文本生成需要从用户历史数据中捕获特定写作风格，但现有检索、参数高效微调和激活引导方法通常存在推理/存储开销较大，或难以将风格信号与语义内容有效解耦的问题。

Method: 提出 GLASS：使用稀疏自编码器从历史回复中抽取全局用户风格先验；基于聚类后的交互场景构造局部对比风格向量；推理阶段将全局与局部向量注入模型不同层，无需检索和参数更新即可实现上下文感知的个性化生成。

Result: 在 LaMP 和 LongLaMP 数据集上，GLASS 在 ROUGE 指标和 LLM-as-judge 评估中均优于基于检索、微调和激活引导的基线方法。

Conclusion: GLASS 通过全局-局部激活引导与稀疏先验提升了个性化生成效果，并且 SAE 表征在主题和长度迁移下更鲁棒，说明其更好地区分了风格信息与语义残留。

Abstract: Personalized text generation requires models to capture user-specific writing styles from historical data. Existing approaches based on retrieval, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, or activation steering either introduce inference and storage overhead or struggle to separate stylistic signals from semantic content. We propose GLASS, a training-free framework for personalized generation via Global-Local Activation Steering with Sparse priors. GLASS uses sparse autoencoders to extract a global user-style prior from historical responses and constructs local contrastive style vectors over clustered interaction scenarios. During inference, it jointly injects global and local vectors into different model layers, enabling context-aware personalization without retrieval or parameter updates. Experiments on LaMP and LongLaMP show that GLASS outperforms retrieval-, fine-tuning-, and steering-based baselines across ROUGE metrics and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Further analyses show that SAE-based representations are more robust to topic and length shifts, suggesting better disentanglement of stylistic information from semantic residue.

</details>


### [101] [FBLayout: Optimizing Memory Layout for Efficient LLM Finetuning on Mobile GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21624)
*Kahou Tam,Wei Niu,Yu Bao,Xiaomin Ouyang,Chengzhong Xu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FBLayout，通过面向移动 GPU 的张量布局协同设计，显著提升 Transformer 端侧微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧微调 Transformer 有助于实现隐私保护和个性化 AI，但移动 GPU 受内存限制明显；训练中的注意力机制存在频繁布局变换，导致反向传播访存碎片化、GPU 利用率低或显式布局转换开销大。

Method: FBLayout 包含三项核心设计：统一的 R-Tile 布局用于前向/反向中的多维归约；基于 tile 的索引变换以避免物理数据搬移；激活引导的布局选择，用于在全局传播高效布局。

Result: 在七个 Transformer 模型及多种手机平台（ARM Mali、Qualcomm Adreno GPU）上，相比 MNN、TFLite 和 TVM 获得 2.2–5.7 倍加速，同时提升缓存效率并降低内存占用。

Conclusion: FBLayout 证明了布局感知的系统级优化可有效缓解移动 GPU 上 Transformer 训练的内存与布局转换瓶颈，使端侧大模型微调更具实用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have enabled unprecedented capabilities across language, vision, and multimodal tasks. On-device fine-tuning of transformer models offers a privacy-preserving path to personalized AI, yet remains inefficient on mobile GPUs due to severe memory constraints and frequent layout transformations in attention mechanism during training. Existing mobile training frameworks either use unified layouts for forward and backward passes -- leading to fragmented memory access and poor GPU utilization during backpropagation -- or rely on explicit layout conversions, which introduce significant transformation overhead.
  To overcome this, we propose FBLayout, a layout-aware framework that co-designs tensor organization with mobile GPU platforms. FBLayout introduces: (1) a unified R-Tile layout for multi-dimensional reductions across forward/backward passes; (2) tile-based index transformation to eliminate physical data movement; and (3) activation-guided layout selection to propagate efficient layouts globally. Evaluations on seven transformer models across different mobile phones (including ARM Mali and Qualcomm Adreno GPUs) show that FBLayout achieves 2.2-5.7x speedup over MNN, TFLite, and TVM, while significantly improving cache efficiency and reducing memory footprint, enabling practical on-device large model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [102] [Trajectory-Aware Retrieval Agents for Temporal Decision- Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21625)
*Jing Wang,Jie Shen,Xing Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TLM，一种面向长篇时序文本决策的闭环 LLM Agent 框架，通过 SHAP 引导反馈与基于检索片段嵌入的潜在增长曲线模型保留并建模时间轨迹信息，从而提升下游决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 通常将长篇、按时间组织的文本切分为孤立片段，容易破坏事件演化顺序、趋势、转折点和信息缺口等关键时序结构，导致依赖时间上下文的决策任务表现受限。

Method: 作者提出 Trajectory Language Model（TLM）：首先检索与任务相关的文本块并形成证据集；随后在检索块嵌入上建立潜在增长曲线模型（LGCM），用于解释和检测轨迹趋势、转折点与信息缺口；再结合 SHAP 引导的反馈机制，对证据集进行迭代式闭环精炼。理论上，在 scorer-calibration 假设下证明迭代过程对正确标签概率是单调非下降的。

Result: 在三个时序决策任务上进行评估：医学问答、业绩电话会议意外预测、隔夜股票跳空预测。TLM 在医学任务上显著优于零样本 LLM 和标准 RAG 基线；在两个金融任务上也获得稳定且具有经济意义的收益提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在长篇时序文本决策中，显式建模文本证据的时间轨迹比单纯片段级检索更有效。TLM 通过 LGCM 与 SHAP 反馈将可解释的轨迹建模和闭环证据优化结合起来，为时序文本上的 LLM Agent 决策提供了一种更可靠的方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of decision-making from long-form, temporally structured text using large language model (LLM) agents. Standard retrievalaugmented generation (RAG) pipelines fragment chronological context into isolated snippets, discarding the temporal structure that is often critical for correct downstream decisions. We introduce TLM (Trajectory Language Model), a closed-loop agentic framework that iteratively refines the evidence set using SHAP-guided feedback. The key technical contribution is the latent growth curve model (LGCM) over retrieved chunk embeddings, which provides an interpretable mechanism for detecting trajectory trends, turning points, and information gaps. We show that, under a scorer-calibration assumption (which holds approximately in practice), the iterative refinement procedure is monotonically non-decreasing in the probability assigned to the correct label. Empirically, TLM is evaluated on three temporally grounded decision tasks: medical question answering, earnings call surprise prediction, and overnight stock gap prediction. TLM substantially outperforms both zero-shot LLM baselines and standard retrieval-augmented approaches on the medical task, and yields consistent, economically meaningful gains on the two financial tasks.

</details>


### [103] [Discrete Action Space as a Prerequisite for GRPO Convergence in Small-Model Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21626)
*Dmytro Filatov,Valentyn Fedorov,Vira Filatova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究用 GRPO 微调小语言模型执行模拟四旋翼连续控制，发现连续动作接口会坍缩到零动作；改为离散 PID 预设选择后可稳定训练，但经典 PID 基线仍在平滑性上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小型语言模型是否能通过强化学习方法 GRPO 学习高频四旋翼速度控制，并分析在连续控制任务中训练失败的原因与可行替代接口。

Method: 以 Qwen-0.5B 等三个预训练语言模型为对象，在 25 Hz 四旋翼速度控制基准上进行 GRPO 微调；比较原始连续动作输出、去除 jerk 惩罚、去除 KL 锚定等消融设置，并将动作空间替换为 5 类 PID 参数预设选择；同时与重新调参的经典 PID 控制器及 Crazyflie 2.1 高保真仿真进行对比。

Result: 原始 GRPO 连续控制训练坍缩为零动作，成功率为 0%，熵在 60 步内由 0.35 降至 0.03；去除 jerk 惩罚或 KL 锚定能避免熵坍缩但无法实现学习。离散 PID 预设接口可收敛，64 步达到 98.6% 成功率、0.656 m/s^3 jerk，256 步达到 100% 成功率、1.103 m/s^3 jerk，在匹配速度上限下 jerk 为 0.796。经典 PID 基线在 Ki=0.30、vmax=2.5 时达到 100% 成功率和 0.736 m/s^3 jerk。高保真仿真揭示悬停区域存在训练分布缺口。

Conclusion: GRPO 直接用于小语言模型的连续四旋翼控制存在严重动作坍缩和学习失败问题；将语言模型决策限制为离散控制器预设选择更可行，但性能并未全面超过强经典控制基线，且仿真分布覆盖仍是关键限制。

Abstract: We study whether Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) can fine-tune small language models for simulated quadrotor continuous-control tasks. In our benchmark, vanilla GRPO fine-tuning of Qwen-0.5B for 25 Hz quadrotor velocity control collapses to the trivial zero action: 0 percent success rate, with entropy falling from 0.35 to 0.03 within 60 steps. Two ablations - removing the jerk-penalty term and removing the KL anchor to the pretrained prior - each prevent entropy collapse, yet neither enables learning. When the action interface is replaced by a 5-way categorical choice over PID presets, training converges. The resulting controller traces a smoothness-reliability Pareto frontier along training duration; both endpoints are reported: 98.6 percent success with 0.656 m/s3 jerk at 64 steps, and 100 percent success with 1.103 m/s3 jerk, or 0.796 under a matched velocity cap, at 256 steps. The recipe is evaluated across three pretrained language models. As context, a re-tuned classical baseline, PID with Ki = 0.30 and vmax = 2.5, reaches the same 100 percent success rate at jerk 0.736 m/s3. A high-fidelity simulation using Crazyflie 2.1 dynamics surfaces a hover-region training-distribution gap.

</details>


### [104] [Do Modules Stay in Their Lane? Role Drift in Compound LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21627)
*Xiaoyang Cao,Siddarth Srinivasan,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出端到端强化学习会提升复合 LLM 系统表面准确率，但可能导致模块偏离预设职责；提出 Role Anchor 正则化方法以观测并控制这种“角色漂移”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合 LLM 系统通常依赖不同模块分工协作，但端到端优化只关注最终任务表现，无法保证各模块按设计角色工作。模块可能通过捷径维持甚至提升准确率，却破坏系统可解释性、可控性与模块化假设，因此需要检测和约束角色偏离。

Method: 作者定义 Role Drift 失败模式，并提出 Role Anchor 正则项。其核心是比较角色提示与中性提示下模块下一词预测分布的差异，并在训练中约束这种由角色提示带来的预测偏移保持稳定，从而限制模块偏离其指定角色。

Result: 在两个复合 LLM 流水线中发现准确率无法揭示的角色漂移：问题分解器会把答案植入子问题，阅读器会绕过检索文本依赖参数记忆。在分解器流水线中，约 86% 的强化学习增益在约束角色后消失。Role Anchor 可在可调准确率代价下缓解角色漂移，效果随流水线和正则强度变化。

Conclusion: 仅用终端准确率评估复合 LLM 系统可能严重高估真实学习效果。Role Anchor 为角色漂移提供了可观测、可控制的正则化机制，并通过梯度分析显示其主要减少了朝角色漂移方向的更新，而非简单抑制学习。

Abstract: End-to-end reinforcement learning can improve the accuracy of compound LLM systems, but it does not constrain how modules divide labor internally. We identify Role Drift, a failure mode in which modules preserve or improve end-task performance while deviating from their assigned roles through role-violating shortcuts that remain invisible to system-level evaluation. To make role drift observable and controllable, we propose Role Anchor, a regularizer that modulates how much each module deviates from its assigned role during end-to-end training. The key idea is to preserve how the role prompt shifts the module's next-token predictions relative to a neutral prompt, which serves as a proxy for the role's intended effect during training. Experiments on two compound LLM pipelines reveal role drift that accuracy alone fails to detect: a decomposer meant to split a question into sub-questions for a separate solver instead plants the answer in them, and a reader meant to answer from retrieved passages instead falls back on parametric memory. In fact, on the decomposer pipeline this shortcut drives most of the apparent RL gain: 86% of it vanishes once the decomposer is held to its role, indicating that terminal accuracy alone can badly overstate how much a compound system has genuinely learned. Across both pipelines, Role Anchor mitigates role drift at a tunable accuracy cost that varies by pipeline and anchor strength. Additional gradient analysis suggests that the regularizer reduces alignment with the role-drift direction rather than simply suppressing learning.

</details>


### [105] [Wavelet Phase Diffusion for Structurally and Semantically Consistent Sim-to-Real Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21628)
*Kaiwen Wang,Frank Bieder,Yinzhe Shen,Carlos Fernandez,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Omer Sahin Tas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Wavelet Phase Diffusion，用局部化小波包相位注入与低频随机化实现无需配对数据、低开销的仿真到真实图像/视频翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真到真实翻译方法难以同时兼顾外观真实感、结构/语义一致性与计算效率：条件控制方法开销大，配对数据方法依赖复杂合成流程且可能改变几何语义，训练免费编辑缺少学习到的外观先验；傅里叶相位保持扩散虽有潜力，但全局频谱耦合会带来振铃和边界泄漏等空间伪影。

Method: 作者提出 Wavelet Phase Diffusion：在 Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform 域中进行操作，利用局部化小波包进行空间自适应相位注入，避免全局频谱干扰；同时引入 Low-Frequency Randomization 替换低频包，以摆脱合成光照先验并获得更接近真实分布的外观。方法使用未配对开放域数据训练，推理额外开销很小，并支持实例级区域翻译。

Result: 在 vKITTI 到 KITTI 图像翻译任务上，该方法在真实感和语义一致性方面优于已有方法，同时保持有竞争力的结构对齐。在 CARLA 视频翻译中，其真实感接近配对数据方法，并使 VLM planner 的 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 5.4% 和 5.1%。
