Conclusion: 该工作表明，相比傅里叶域相位保持扩散，基于局部小波包的相位扩散更适合保持结构与语义一致性，同时提升真实感；低频随机化进一步增强了真实域外观建模能力，使方法在无需配对数据且低推理开销的条件下实现高质量仿真到真实翻译。

Abstract: Simulation-to-reality translation must bridge the appearance gap between synthetic and real domains while preserving structural and semantic consistency. Conditioning-based methods achieve spatial alignment but introduce computationally expensive control modules. Paired-data methods achieve realism but rely on complex synthesis pipelines, often altering scene geometry and semantics. Training-free editing methods avoid both constraints but lack a learned appearance prior, limiting their perceptual quality. Recently proposed phase-preserving diffusion presents a promising alternative, but Fourier-domain formulations are constrained by global spectral coupling. This coupling induces spatial artifacts such as ringing and boundary leakage, thereby degrading structural and semantic consistency. We introduce Wavelet Phase Diffusion, which addresses this through two components. First, we operate in the Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform domain, whose localized wavelet packets enable spatially adaptive phase injection without global spectral interference. Second, Low-Frequency Randomization (LFR) replaces the low-frequency packet, decoupling the model from the synthetic illumination prior and enabling in-distribution real-world appearance. Both components train on unpaired open-domain data, and introduce negligible inference overhead. The spatial locality further enables instance-level translation, where individual objects or regions are translated to photorealistic appearance independently while the surrounding scene remains untranslated. On vKITTI $\to$ KITTI image translation, ours outperforms prior methods in realism and semantic consistency while maintaining competitive structural alignment. For CARLA video translation, ours approaches the realism of paired-data methods while reducing VLM planner ADE and FDE by $5.4\%$ and $5.1\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [106] [Defining AI-Native Systems: Autonomy as Revision Authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21659)
*Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为“AI-native”系统提出技术定义：关键不在模型能力，而在 AI 是否拥有修改系统自身实现的自主修订权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 已开始生成、验证和部署系统代码，但“AI-native”长期停留在营销概念，缺乏精确、可操作的技术定义。

Method: 论文基于系统的决策层级模型，区分决策执行者（occupancy）与修订权限（revision authority），并构建从自调优、自重写到自架构的权限阶梯。

Result: 作者将 AI-native 定义为：AI 能够自主重写系统自身实现的系统；同时要求具备升级检测器、验证流程和经验证的回退机制。

Conclusion: AI-native 的本质是 AI 对系统自身决策与实现的修订权，而非底层模型能力；但系统目标与正确性边界仍由人类掌控。

Abstract: AI has begun to write systems code: agents now synthesize, verify, and deploy system components. Despite this shift, "AI-native" remains a marketing term with no precise technical definition. This paper gives it one. We define AI-nativeness along a single axis---authority over the system's own decisions rather than by the capability of the underlying AI models. Building on a decision-level model of a system, we distinguish occupancy (who executes a decision) from revision authority (who may change it), organize revision authority into a ladder---self-tuning, self-rewriting, self-architecting and define a system as AI-native when an AI autonomously rewrites the system's own implementations. The definition further requires an escalation detector, a verification procedure, and a verified fallback, while leaving purpose and correctness human-owned.

</details>


### [107] [Persistent Computational State: A Session-Centric Runtime for Generative World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21686)
*Zhen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，许多生成式视频世界模型并非缺乏“返回已访问状态后继续一致生成”的能力，问题主要来自请求中心化服务框架丢弃了运行时不可重算状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准认为视频世界模型在规划场景中无法正确支持状态分叉、未来展开、回溯与返回，并将失败归因于模型架构或训练目标。作者质疑这一归因是否完整，尤其关注运行时状态管理是否才是关键瓶颈。

Method: 作者分析并实测不同架构中运行时已有的状态快照与恢复机制，包括观测加随机数状态、记忆库、窗口化 KV 上下文等；提出 Persistent Computational State（PCS）概念，即跨请求必须保存的最小不可重算状态，并构建基于 PCS 的会话中心化运行时。

Result: 在三类模型上，保存并恢复相应运行时状态后，可以逐字节复现“从未离开”的后续生成；仅破坏随机数状态会导致性能下降。检查点与恢复开销仅 0.012 ms，而单步生成约 1.85 s；常驻会话主要受主机资源而非设备资源限制，实测可达 1,024 个。

Conclusion: 世界模型的关键问题不一定是模型能力缺失，而是部署范式错误：沿用语言模型的请求中心化服务假设会丢弃世界模型所需的不可重算运行时状态。应采用会话中心化运行时，并按与返回相关性而非最近使用原则管理世界记忆。

Abstract: Generative world models are increasingly driven as simulators: a planner forks a state, rolls out futures, backtracks, and returns to a visited viewpoint. Recent benchmarks establish that current video world models fail this usage, and attribute it to the model, prescribing new architectures and training objectives. We show this attribution is incomplete, and for an important class of models simply wrong. Snapshotting the state the runtime already holds -- an observation plus RNG state, a memory bank, or a windowed KV context, by architecture -- and restoring it after a genuine excursion reproduces the never-left continuation byte-identically on all three; corrupting only the RNG degrades it. The capability was never missing: request-centric serving discarded it, inheriting from language-model serving the assumption that runtime state is recomputable -- but world-model state carries a non-recomputable kernel. We define Persistent Computational State (PCS), the minimal non-recomputable state that must survive across requests, show it can be discovered by measurement, and build a session-centric runtime over it. Checkpoint and restore cost 0.012 ms against a 1.85 s generation step; resident sessions become host- rather than device-bounded (measured to 1,024); and world memory must be evicted by relevance to the return, not recency -- the inverse of LLM practice.

</details>


### [108] [What AI Red-Team Evaluations Can and Cannot Prove](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21735)
*Bandana Kaur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“评估证据上限”概念，用于量化固定测试预算下红队/安全评测结果最多能在多大程度上改变信念，并给出闭式边界来判断评测能支持哪些安全性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI红队评测常被用来支持模型安全或不安全的主张，但哪些主张能由评测数据有效支持、哪些不能，往往依赖主观判断。论文希望建立一个可计算的证据边界，明确评测在不同风险发生率下的证明能力。

Method: 作者定义评估的evidential ceiling，在固定测试预算、固定评分规则和近似独立试验结构下，为基准测试的零失败结果推导闭式形式；进一步用假设条件下的诱发率扩展到自适应和自动化红队，并审计八个评测套件相对于该边界的位置。

Result: 研究发现：当危害率高于某个可计算阈值时，中等规模基准测试可达到指定证据标准，且零失败结果比单个复现失败更有证据力；低于该阈值时， feasible规模的被动基准测试无法提供足够安全性证据。现有基准对高频危害类别较充分，但对罕见灾难性类别差数个数量级。

Conclusion: 安全基准测试并非没有信息量，但其信息量只覆盖一组特定且可计算的命题。AI安全评测需要明确声明其能够证明的风险频率范围和证据标准，而不是笼统宣称模型安全。

Abstract: Red-team evaluations of AI models support some claims and not others, and the boundary between the two is calculable rather than merely a matter of judgment. We define the evidential ceiling of an evaluation as the largest factor by which one result can move belief under a fixed testing budget, derive it in closed form for the benchmark null result, and use it to locate that boundary exactly. We find that above a calculable harm rate, a benchmark of modest size certifies a category to a stated evidentiary standard, and a clean sheet is then the stronger of the two possible observations, outweighing a single reproduced failure. Below that rate, no passive benchmark of feasible size provides the specified evidence of safety under the fixed scoring rule and approximately independent trial structure. The crossing between the two regimes has a closed form. The bound is not specific to benchmarks: written in terms of a procedure's hypothesis conditioned elicitation rates, it covers adaptive and automated red teaming as well, and shows that discrimination between the hypotheses rather than attack success is what determines evidential worth. Auditing eight evaluation suites against the boundary, we find that current benchmarks are adequate for high-frequency harm categories and several orders of magnitude short for rare, catastrophic ones. Safety benchmarks are not uninformative. They are informative about a specific and computable set of propositions, and the discipline they need is to state which.

</details>


### [109] [From Seasonality to Semantics: Benchmarking a Hybrid Probabilistic Forecasting System for Roadblocks in Bolivia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21785)
*Rodrigo Vargas Sainz,Christian Berón Curti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Roadblocks in Bolivia are a social conflict phenomenon with devastating economic impacts, estimated at losses equivalent to 4% of the national Gross Domestic Product. Despite their recurrence and impact, there is a lack of local predictive systems to anticipate these events for logistical decision-making. This paper presents a hybrid probabilistic forecasting system that integrates time series decomposition (Prophet) with natural language processing (NLP) techniques applied to a six-year corpus of Bolivian news coverage. The methodology employs vector semantic embeddings and zero-shot classification models to capture signals of discursive escalation prior to the materialization of the roadblocks. Using an expanding walk-forward validation scheme applied over 1,762 days and seven forecasting horizons (H+1 to H+7), seven internal configurations and four external benchmarks were compared, including SARIMA and LightGBM. The results demonstrate that the hybrid configuration (Prophet + NLP, C6) consistently outperforms purely statistical models, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.677 at H+1 and reducing the Brier Score by 10.9% relative to the baseline temporal model (0.220 vs. 0.247), maintaining a statistically significant error reduction across all evaluated horizons ($p < 0.02$). This research validates that the integration of semantic news signals allows for the detection of social tension peaks not captured by historical inertia, providing a technical tool for risk management in critical transport corridors.

</details>


### [110] [QLPO: Quadrant-weighted Sampling for Length-aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21793)
*Siwei Chen,Siqi Chen,Xupeng Miao,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 QLPO，一种基于重采样的长度感知策略优化方法，可在不修改奖励函数的情况下显著缩短推理模型输出长度，并基本保持推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在强化学习训练后常产生冗长的 chain-of-thought，导致推理延迟高、部署成本增加。现有长度控制方法通常依赖显式长度惩罚或额外控制模块，需要复杂调参，且可能损害推理质量。

Method: QLPO 是 GRPO 的变体：先过量生成候选回答，再按经验正确/错误比例重采样训练组；重采样时偏向保留较短的正确回答和较长的错误回答，从而重塑训练分布，隐式鼓励模型生成更短输出，而不改变奖励函数。

Result: 在 1.5B 到 32B 参数规模的模型上，包括基础模型和强推理模型，QLPO 均改善了准确率与输出长度之间的权衡；响应长度减少约 30% 到 70%，同时保持推理性能。

Conclusion: 结构化重采样是一种简单、稳健且有效的高效推理优化策略，能够降低推理成本并避免显式长度惩罚带来的调参和性能风险。

Abstract: Recent large reasoning models often develop long chain-of-thought responses during reinforcement learning (RL), resulting in high inference latency and deployment cost. Existing methods for response length control typically rely on explicit length penalties or additional control modules, which require careful tuning and may compromise reasoning quality. We propose Quadrant-weighted Sampling for Length-aware Policy Optimization (QLPO), a simple resampling-based variant of GRPO that introduces implicit length control without modifying the reward function. QLPO first over-generates candidate responses and then resamples the training group by preserving the empirical correct/incorrect ratio while favoring short correct responses and long incorrect responses. This reshapes the training distribution and implicitly encourages shorter model outputs. Across models ranging from 1.5B to 32B parameters, including both base models and strong reasoning models, QLPO consistently improves the accuracy-length trade-off. It reduces response length by 30% to 70% while preserving reasoning performance. These results suggest that structured resampling provides an effective and robust approach to efficient reasoning.

</details>


### [111] [DAGForge: Auditable Causal DAG Authoring with Biomedical Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21859)
*Yi-han Sheu,Michael R. Steigman,Yu Zhou,Bo Wang,Fan-Yu Yen,Jordan W. Smoller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAGForge 是一个面向生物医学因果分析的浏览器系统，可基于研究概念描述自动检索并固定文献证据，利用 LLM 生成可审计的成对因果判断，并组装为带约束检查的因果 DAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学因果 DAG 构建通常依赖人工完成，研究者需要查阅文献、判断不确定的因果关系，并保留足够的证据来源以供专家复核；该过程耗时、主观且难以审计。

Method: 系统从自由文本研究概念出发，生成可复现的文献快照；通过基于 LLM 的推理模块提取逐字证据片段并形成结构化的成对因果判断；随后将判断组装为经过约束检查的图，并为每条边提供置信度、出处和可复核理由。界面支持研究设定、进度监控、证据审查、图比较、调整集计算与导出。

Result: 在紧凑基准 DAG 和来自已发表文献的参考 DAG 上评估时，DAGForge 在文献型队列中实现了较高的边召回率，并相比纯 LLM 基线保留了可验证的证据链。

Conclusion: DAGForge 降低了因果 DAG 整理与构建的人工负担，同时增强了因果假设的可审计性，有助于生物医学研究的设计、分析和解释。

Abstract: Constructing causal directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) is a core step in biomedical causal analysis, yet it remains a largely manual process. Analysts must connect study variables to prior literature, evaluate uncertain causal claims, and preserve sufficient provenance for expert review. We present DAGForge, a browser-based system for authoring causal DAGs as auditable, evidence-linked artifacts. Given free-text descriptions of study concepts, DAGForge creates a reproducible literature snapshot, uses an LLM-based reasoning module to generate structured pairwise causal judgments grounded in verbatim evidence excerpts, and assembles those judgments into a constraint-checked graph. Each proposed edge includes confidence estimates, provenance, and a reviewable rationale. The interface supports study specification, progress monitoring, evidence review, graph comparison, adjustment-set computation, and export. In evaluations against both compact benchmark DAGs and reference DAGs derived from published literature, DAGForge achieves high edge recall on the literature-based cohort while retaining verifiable evidence trails absent from LLM-only baselines. DAGForge thus reduces the burden of causal DAG curation while making the resulting assumptions auditable, supporting the design, analysis, and interpretation of biomedical studies.

</details>


### [112] [When Is a Learned Command Adapter Worth It? Closed-Loop Identification and Counterfactual Auditing of Frozen Locomotion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21867)
*Zongtan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“适配器必要性审计”框架，用于判断在冻结的命令条件化运动策略上加入学习型适配器是否真正有部署价值；实验显示多数真实部署场景下证据不足或不支持加入适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型适配器常被用于提升冻结运动控制策略的性能，但其收益可能只是离线或学习器层面的表象，未必能由部署时可观测状态恢复并转化为真实闭环收益。因此需要一个审计方法，区分真实可部署收益、同状态反事实潜力、状态分配收益以及约束违规风险。

Method: 作者提出适配器必要性审计，将收益分解为全局操作点增益、同状态反事实余量、相对于交叉拟合固定动作的部署增益、相对于频率匹配随机策略的状态分配增益，并通过源簇学习器重拟合将这些指标与约束违规映射为GO/NO-GO/ABSTAIN决策。还可加入闭环命令-响应辨识特征。实验在Go2平台上，对direct、scale、heading、yaw干预及direct control、VGCC、MPC诱导的查询分布进行二十独立簇和200次完整学习器重拟合的确认性审计。

Result: 归档scale-prefix诊断显示5.2%的同状态headroom，但实际恢复的allocation gain仅0.55%。在1%部署收益和分配收益阈值、5%违规容忍度下，direct queries得到NO-GO，VGCC和MPC得到ABSTAIN。VGCC平均部署增益最高为1.34%，但allocation下界仅0.09%，违规上界达6.25%。部署代表性的二十簇H1审计返回NO-GO，而学习器级synthetic control返回GO。

Conclusion: 该审计框架强调：适配器是否值得加入应由部署时可观测信号是否能支持状态依赖自适应来决定，而不能仅凭离线潜在提升或学习器层面的结果假定适配器有价值。

Abstract: Adding a learned adapter to a frozen, command-conditioned locomotion policy is worthwhile only if the interface exposes improvements that are both real and recoverable from deployment-time observations. We introduce an adapter necessity audit that separates global operating-point gain,same-state counterfactual headroom, deployment gain over a cross-fitted fixed action, and state-allocation gain over a frequency-matched randomized policy. Source-cluster learner refits map these quantities and constraint violations to a GO/NO-GO/ABSTAIN decision. Closed-loop command- response identification provides optional decision features. On Go2, an archived scale-prefix diagnostic finds 5.2% same-state headroom but only 0.55% recovered allocation gain. Our confirmatory audit evaluates direct, scale, heading, and yaw interventions on twenty independent clusters for each of three query distributions induced by direct control, VGCC, and MPC, using 200 full learner refits. At 1% deployment and allocation thresholds and a 5% violation tolerance, direct queries return NO-GO, while VGCC and MPC queries ABSTAIN. VGCC has the largest mean deployment gain (1.34%), but its allocation lower bound is 0.09% and its violation upper bound is 6.25%. A deployment-representative twenty-cluster H1 audit also returns NO-GO, whereas a learner-level synthetic control returns GO. The audit therefore tests whether observable signal justifies state-dependent adaptation rather than presuming that an adapter is valuable.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-Agent System-driven Digital Twins for predictive maintenance: architectures, technologies and open research challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21873)
*Korota Arsène Coulibaly,Mohamed Hamlich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统综述，聚焦多智能体系统与数字孪生在预测性维护中的融合，尤其关注资源受限工业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生在工业4.0中已成为实时虚拟表征物理系统的关键技术，但分布式工业环境中的复杂性增长带来了自主决策、动态适应和智能体协同方面的架构挑战。

Method: 作者系统分析了547余篇发表于高影响力期刊的论文，构建混合架构分类体系，识别技术瓶颈，并提出面向嵌入式AI、轻量级通信协调和层级化数字孪生编排的开放研究问题。

Result: 研究发现，尽管已有大量进展，现有方案仍未能同时实现嵌入式、分布式和层级化的一体化架构，尤其难以满足资源受限条件下预测性维护和智能工厂控制的综合需求。

Conclusion: 该综述指出未来研究应面向工业5.0需求，发展可解释AI、剩余寿命估计、轻量级分布式协同和资源受限设备上的智能部署，以形成完整的嵌入式—分布式—层级化数字孪生解决方案。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a foundational technology within the context of Industry 4.0, offering a paradigm for the real-time virtual representation of physical systems. However, managing their growing complexity, particularly in distributed industrial environments, requires intelligent architectures capable of autonomous decision-making, dynamic adaptability, and inter-agent coordination. This systematic review explores the intersection between Multi-Agent Systems and Digital Twins, with a particular focus on predictive maintenance applications in resource-constrained contexts. Through a critical analysis of over 547 papers published in high-impact journals (IEEE Transactions, Nature, Elsevier, MDPI), we establish a taxonomy of existing hybrid architectures, identify persistent technological bottlenecks, and formulate three open research questions concerning: (i) the deployment of artificial intelligence on resource-constrained microcontrollers, (ii) distributed multi-node coordination via lightweight communication protocols, and (iii) the hierarchical orchestration of Digital Twins toward smart factory control integrating residual life estimation and explainable Artificial Intelligence. The results of this analysis reveal that, despite significant progress, no existing system offers an integrated embedded-distributed hierarchical solution that simultaneously meets the requirements of Industry 5.0.

</details>


### [114] [TRW: TRACE-RealWorld---An Auditable Consistency Contract for World Models as Materialized Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21910)
*Edward Y. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-RealWorld 提出一种面向动态物理世界的物化预测视图维护框架，强调一致性、恢复与可追责性，而非新的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续变化的现实环境中，基础状态读取往往存在成本高、延迟大、数据源异构且可能失败等问题，因此需要一种机制来维护可操作的物化视图，并保证其在授权、执行和后续验证中的有效性。

Method: 论文引入承诺级有效性抽象来管理物化预测；采用基于后果条件的自适应视图维护策略；使用事务式、依赖范围限定的补偿机制处理授权后失效的承诺；并通过追加式 provenance 支持精确重放。其方法结合了物化视图维护、自适应流同步、事务恢复、saga、数据新鲜度与 provenance 等思想。

Result: 在 Flood-SAR 端到端评估中，论文将感知视为物理数据采集，并围绕六个预注册问题和保留随机种子评估新鲜度、验证成本、陈旧读取、恢复范围、恢复失败以及可重放性。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是为学习型世界表示作为运行时数据系统部署提供一致性、恢复和问责契约，而不是改进或提出新的预测模型。

Abstract: TRACE-RealWorld addresses a core data-management problem: maintaining an actionable materialized view over a continuously changing physical world when reads of the base state are priced, delayed, heterogeneous, and fallible. Its data-management contributions are a commitment-level validity abstraction for materialized predictions; consequence-conditioned adaptive view maintenance; transaction-style, dependency-scoped compensation for commitments invalidated after authorization; and append-only provenance supporting exact replay. The work builds directly on materialized-view maintenance, adaptive stream synchronization, transaction recovery, sagas, data freshness, and provenance. The end-to-end Flood-SAR evaluation treats sensing as physical data acquisition and measures freshness, verification cost, stale reads, recovery scope, restoration failure, and replayability through six pre-registered questions with held-out seeds. The contribution is therefore not a new predictive model, but a consistency, recovery, and accountability contract for deploying learned world representations as operational data systems.

</details>


### [115] [Semiotic logical hexagon theory for LLM logical reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21933)
*Yunyao Zhang,Xinglang Zhang,Zeliang Chen,Junqing Yu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HexLogicAgent：先组织自然语言陈述中的隐含语义关系，再通过结构化验证引导逻辑推理，从而提升大语言模型在复杂逻辑任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要同时理解语义与执行逻辑推理的问题上仍容易出错。作者认为关键瓶颈不只是演绎推理能力不足，而是自然语言中隐含的语义关系未被完整组织，导致后续看似有效的推理建立在不完整或错误的语义基础上。

Method: 提出 HexLogicAgent 框架，先对自然语言语句的意义进行组织，显式建模语义对立关系的完整结构，再使用结构化验证来指导逻辑推理。其理论依据来自逻辑六边形理论，用于解释为何完整的对立意义结构对可靠推理是必要的。

Result: 在具有挑战性的逻辑推理基准上，HexLogicAgent 在多个大语言模型上持续提升推理可靠性。实验观察表明，不完整的语义表示是推理失败的重要来源；完整建模语义对立结构能在逻辑复杂度增加时显著延缓性能退化。

Conclusion: 该研究强调语义组织是大语言模型逻辑推理可靠性的关键前提。通过在推理前显式构建完整语义对立结构，HexLogicAgent 能提升复杂逻辑任务中的稳定性与正确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become powerful tools for language understanding and logical reasoning. However, they still make mistakes when a problem requires both understanding meaning and following logic. A key reason is that natural-language statements often carry implicit semantic relations before any formal reasoning begins. If these hidden meanings are not properly organized, the model may reach incorrect conclusions even when the subsequent reasoning process appears logically valid. Existing methods improve reasoning through decomposition, symbolic translation, external solvers, or self-verification, but pay comparatively less attention to the semantic structure on which reasoning depends. In this paper, we further investigate how semantic organization influences logical reasoning in LLMs. To this end, we propose HexLogicAgent, a framework that first organizes the meaning of natural-language statements and then guides logical reasoning through structured verification. In our investigation, we also make two observations. First, incomplete semantic representations, rather than deductive inference itself, are a major source of logical reasoning failures in LLMs. Second, explicitly modeling the complete structure of semantic opposition substantially delays the degradation of reasoning performance as logical complexity increases. Experiments on challenging logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that HexLogicAgent consistently improves reasoning reliability across multiple LLMs. The core idea is supported by a logical hexagon theory, which explains why a complete structure of opposing meanings is necessary for reliable reasoning.

</details>


### [116] [Learning as Reasoning Unfolds: Progressive Rollout Allocation for Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22002)
*Heyang Jiang,Henry Liu,Baharan Mirzasoleiman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VIGOR通过基于奖励方差的在线动态rollout分配，减少GRPO中大量低信息量长链式推理采样带来的训练开销，并在数学与代码任务上以更少rollout达到或超过GRPO性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等RLVR方法依赖为每个样本生成固定数量的长CoT rollouts，训练成本随rollout数量线性增长，且许多rollout对梯度更新贡献有限，导致计算昂贵、训练不稳定。现有过滤策略虽能提升效率，但往往需要额外生成候选或依赖历史信号，整体加速有限。

Method: 论文提出VarIance Guided Online Rollout allocation（VIGOR）：在每个batch中先为所有样本分配少量rollouts，然后根据组内奖励方差迭代地将额外rollout预算分配给方差最高、信息量更大的样本，直到达到固定总预算。理论上分析RLVR中奖励方差对梯度幅度的控制作用，并推导相对GRPO的闭式加速比。

Result: 在数学推理任务中，VIGOR最多可用少2.3倍的rollouts达到目标准确率；在代码任务中，用少1.49倍的rollouts达到GRPO最终full pass rate，并将平均测试通过率提升3.4个百分点。

Conclusion: VIGOR表明，在RLVR训练中按奖励方差自适应分配采样预算比固定rollout分配更高效，可显著降低计算成本并保持甚至提升性能，是改进GRPO类方法训练效率和稳定性的有效方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a highly effective framework for improving LLM reasoning, with methods such as GRPO among its most successful instantiations. However, GRPO relies on repeated generation of long chain-of-thought rollouts. Training time scales with the number of rollouts, a large fraction of which are uninformative. Thus, GRPO is computationally expensive and unstable. To mitigate this, existing approaches either generate a larger pool of rollouts and filter the most informative prompts, or leverage historical signals for filtering at later stages of training. These strategies offer modest performance gains, but slow down the overall process. To address this, we propose VarIance Guided Online Rollout allocation (VIGOR) which instead of allocating a fixed rollout budget per example, begins with a small number of rollouts for all examples in a batch and iteratively allocates additional rollouts to those with the highest group reward variance until a fixed total rollout budget is reached. Theoretically, we show that under RLVR, reward variance controls the gradient magnitude, and derive VIGOR's closed-form speedup ratio over GRPO, which grows with refinement rounds under Pareto-distributed reward variance. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and coding tasks show that VIGOR reaches target accuracy with up to 2.3$\times$ fewer rollouts on math, reaches GRPO's final coding full pass rate with 1.49$\times$ fewer rollouts, and improves the coding average test pass rate by 3.4 points.

</details>


### [117] [Zero-Shot Mission-Level Evaluation for Aerial MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22014)
*Suman Navaratnarajah,Taehyoung Kim,Jona Ruthardt,Ishaan Bhimwal,Ryousuke Yamada,Yannik Blei,Wolfram Burgard,Yuki M Asano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MissionBench，用于评估多模态大语言模型在空中三维环境中的任务级具身能力；当前最强模型成功率低于35%，显著落后于人类84.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 MLLM 正逐渐成为具身智能体的核心推理模块，但其是否能在无需特定微调的情况下，仅凭单条高层指令完成长时程具身任务仍不明确。因此需要一个面向任务级、闭环、多步骤决策的评测基准。

Method: 作者构建 MissionBench，包含5个模拟三维环境、4类任务族、共120个任务。智能体只能使用第一视角观测和动作历史，自主完成规划、导航与结果汇报，并在无空中特定微调的条件下评估22个开源与闭源 MLLM。

Result: 最强模型任务成功率不足35%，而人类表现为84.4%。不同模型家族间差异明显；模型规模增大带来一定性能提升，说明较大通用模型具备更强的零样本具身能力。

Conclusion: 任务级具身能力不仅依赖空间感知，还需要多步规划、自适应推理和闭环决策协调。MissionBench 揭示了当前 MLLM 在长时程具身任务中的显著不足，同时表明规模化可能带来能力提升，也伴随具身 AI 发展的潜在风险。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are emerging as core reasoning modules for embodied agents, yet it remains unclear how well general-purpose models can solve long-horizon embodied tasks from a single high-level instruction. We introduce MissionBench, a benchmark for mission-level evaluation of MLLMs in aerial 3D environments. It comprises 120 missions across five simulated 3D environments and four task families. Agents must autonomously plan, navigate, and report outcomes using only egocentric observations and its action history, without aerial-specific fine-tuning. Across 22 open- and closed-source MLLMs, the strongest model succeeds on fewer than 35% of missions compared to 84.4% human performance, highlighting the difficulty of multi-step embodied tasks. Despite large variations between model families, we observe gains from scaling, indicating that larger general-purpose models possess stronger zero-shot embodied capabilities. Our analysis shows that mission-level competence requires coordinating multiple capabilities beyond spatial perception, including multi-step planning and adaptive reasoning. This motivates closed-loop evaluation and highlights both the promise and risk of scaling-driven improvements for embodied AI.

</details>


### [118] [Nanbeige4.2-3B: Unlocking Agentic Capabilities in a Compact Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22083)
*Nanbeige Lab,:,Chen Yang,Chengrui Huang,Fufeng Lan,Hanhui Chen,Hao Zhou,Huatong Song,Jiaqi Cao,Jiaying Zhu,Jinlin Niu,Kai Wang,Lisheng Huang,Qiliang Liang,Ran Le,Ruixiang Feng,Shuang Sun,Tao Gu,Tao Zhang,Tianyu Luo,Yang Song,Yun Xing,Yuntao Wen,Ziyao Xu,Zongchao Chen,Zongqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nanbeige4.2-3B 是一个仅 3B 非嵌入参数的紧凑型通用智能体模型，通过 Looped Transformer、大规模预训练、多样化 SFT 轨迹构建与多阶段强化学习，在代码智能体、办公智能体、复杂工具调用及推理任务上取得较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用智能体模型往往参数规模较大、部署成本高，难以作为本地个人助手高效运行。该工作旨在构建一个小参数规模但具备强工具使用、长程任务执行和通用推理能力的紧凑模型。

Method: 模型从零开始在 28T tokens 上预训练，采用 Looped Transformer 复用层堆栈以在不增加参数的情况下提升容量；在 SFT 阶段通过真实部署和大规模合成扩展可执行环境、任务资产与智能体脚手架；强化学习阶段结合混合模式 RLHF、长度受控推理 RL，以及带结果奖励和过程奖励的智能体 RL，以提升质量、效率并稳定长程训练。

Result: 实验显示，Nanbeige4.2-3B 在多类智能体基准上超过更大规模模型，包括 Qwen3.5-9B 和 Gemma4-12B，同时在数学、代码、科学推理和对齐任务上保持竞争力；结合 OpenClaw 的表现也支持其作为紧凑型本地个人助手使用。

Conclusion: 该论文表明，通过结构设计、数据与轨迹多样化以及针对智能体任务优化的强化学习流程，小规模模型也可以获得强智能体能力和较好的推理表现，具备本地化部署价值。

Abstract: We present Nanbeige4.2-3B, a compact general agentic model with 3B non-embedding parameters. It delivers strong performance across code-agent, office-agent, and complex tool-use tasks while maintaining highly competitive reasoning capabilities in mathematics, coding, and science. Nanbeige4.2-3B is pretrained from scratch on 28T tokens with a Looped Transformer that reuses the layer stack to increase capacity without adding parameters. For SFT data and trajectory construction, we expand the diversity of executable environments, task assets, and agentic scaffolds through real-world deployment and large-scale synthesis. Our RL pipeline applies mixed-mode RLHF over Think and Non-Think responses to improve overall model quality and reduce failure cases, length-controlled reasoning RL to balance accuracy and reasoning efficiency, and agentic RL with outcome and process rewards to stabilize long-horizon training. Extensive evaluations show that Nanbeige4.2-3B outperforms larger models, including Qwen3.5-9B and Gemma4-12B, across diverse agentic benchmarks while remaining competitive on reasoning and alignment tasks. Performance with OpenClaw further supports its use as a compact local personal assistant.

</details>


### [119] [Reasoning Denoiser: Denoising Reasoning Traces for Hallucination Detection in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22098)
*Junlin Fang,Do Nguyen-Thanh,Xiaogang Xu,Zhen Fang,Sean Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 REDE，用最终答案注意力作为自动监督来学习步骤级表示，过滤大推理模型推理轨迹中的无关与重复噪声，从而提升幻觉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在回答前会生成很长的推理轨迹，其中包含有助于判断答案真实性的信号；但长轨迹中常混有无关步骤和重复步骤，这些噪声会掩盖与幻觉检测相关的有效线索。现有基于置信度的分数和朴素嵌入过滤方法难以稳定区分噪声步骤与信息步骤。

Method: 作者首先识别并分析两类主要推理噪声：无关步骤与重复步骤。随后提出 REDE 框架，利用最终答案注意力作为自动监督信号，塑造步骤级表示空间，学习更适合区分噪声与有效推理步骤的精炼嵌入；在检测时先过滤噪声步骤，再将清洗后的推理轨迹接入不同幻觉检测器。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的实验表明，无关和重复步骤会显著降低幻觉检测效果；REDE 在多种幻觉检测器和竞争性基线之上均能带来稳定性能提升。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中的噪声是影响大推理模型幻觉检测的重要因素。通过基于最终答案注意力的表示学习进行轨迹去噪，REDE 能更可靠地保留真实性评估相关信息，并作为通用插件提升幻觉检测能力。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate long reasoning traces before producing final answers. While these traces may contain useful signals for hallucination detection, harnessing them is non-trivial because long trajectories often include noisy steps that obscure the cues relevant to truthfulness assessment. In this paper, we identify two prevalent forms of reasoning noises, i.e., irrelevant steps and repetitive steps, and show that both substantially degrade hallucination detection performance. Existing confidence-based scores and naive embedding-based filtering fail to reliably separate noisy from informative steps. To address this challenge, we propose REDE, a novel learning framework for denoising reasoning traces for hallucination detection. Specifically, REDE leverages final-answer attention as an automatic supervision signal to shape the step-level representation space, yielding refined embeddings in which noisy steps can be reliably identified and filtered. REDE can be readily plugged into diverse hallucination detectors by operating on the filtered reasoning trajectory after removing noisy steps. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks show that REDE consistently improves detection performance over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Industrial Tokenization for LLM-Based Health Intelligence: A Federated Architecture for Industrial Evidence Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22153)
*Deshui Li,Xiao-Ming Yuan,Zishun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“工业令牌化”概念，将不同工业信息源的分析输出转化为结构化、可机器解释的工业证据单元，以支持大语言模型或智能体进行可追溯、可解释的跨源推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业健康管理依赖状态监测、SCADA、维护记录、检查结果和预测模型等异构来源。由于这些数据在结构、时间分辨率、物理含义和可靠性上差异显著，直接整合进单一模型会损害解释性、可追溯性以及对设备和数据变化的适应能力。

Method: 提出 Industrial Tokenization 作为语义接口，将源特定分析输出封装为 Industrial Tokens，包含证据来源、时间范围、运行上下文、分析含义、质量/置信度和溯源信息；进一步设计联邦式工业架构，使各分析子系统保持自治并向中央推理层暴露标准化令牌。初步实现聚焦于基于振动诊断输出的 DiagnosisToken 路径，包括规则事件聚合、结构化文本令牌生成和基于 LLM 的解释。

Result: 论文给出了一个端到端 DiagnosisToken 初始实现，展示了振动诊断结果如何被组织为结构化工业证据，并交由 LLM 进行解释。其他令牌类型如 SCADA 状态监测令牌、维护令牌和预测令牌被作为未来扩展方向。

Conclusion: 工业令牌化被定位为连接领域特定工业智能与 LLM/智能体推理的语义接口，而不是原始工业数据编码方法。该框架强调模块自治、标准化证据表达、可解释性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Industrial health management increasingly relies on heterogeneous information sources, including condition monitoring systems, supervisory control and data acquisition systems, maintenance records, inspection results, and prognostic models. Although large language models provide new opportunities for cross-source reasoning, industrial data and analytical outputs differ substantially in structure, temporal resolution, physical meaning, and reliability. Directly integrating such heterogeneous information into a monolithic model may reduce interpretability, traceability, and adaptability to equipment and data changes. This paper introduces Industrial Tokenization, a conceptual interface for transforming source-specific analytical outputs into structured and machine-interpretable units of industrial evidence, termed Industrial Tokens. Unlike numerical tokens used to encode raw time-series data, Industrial Tokens represent domain-grounded evidence together with source, temporal scope, operating context, analytical meaning, quality or confidence information, and provenance. Based on this concept, a federated industrial architecture is proposed, where heterogeneous analytical subsystems retain autonomy while exposing standardized Industrial Tokens to a central reasoning layer. As an initial implementation, this study presents an end-to-end DiagnosisToken pathway based on vibration-diagnostic outputs, rule-based event aggregation, structured textual token generation, and LLM-based interpretation. Other Industrial Tokens, including SCADA-based condition-monitoring tokens, maintenance tokens, and prognostic tokens, are reserved as future extensions. The proposed framework positions Industrial Tokenization as a semantic interface between domain-specific industrial intelligence and LLM- or agent-based reasoning, rather than another method for encoding raw industrial data.

</details>


### [121] [Learning on the Job: Continual Learning from Deployment Feedback for Frozen-Weights Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22157)
*Valentin Tablan,Scott Taylor,Kristoffer Bernhem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用外部自然语言规则记忆，让冻结部署的AI代理利用每次运行后的结果反馈或纠错实现持续学习，在τ-bench银行领域显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的AI代理通常冻结底层模型，导致其在完成困难请求后无法把经验累积到后续相似任务中；但实际运行过程会自然产生结果判定和事后纠错，这些反馈可能成为低成本持续学习信号。

Method: 将冻结模型与外部记忆模块结合，把每次交互经验蒸馏为可检索的自然语言规则；在后续任务中检索这些规则辅助决策。实验在τ-bench银行领域进行，并与检索完整策略语料的静态RAG基线比较；反馈信号包括一位结果判定和更丰富的纠错信息。

Result: 基于一位结果判定的学习使单次成功率达到基线的1.6倍；基于纠错的学习达到2.6倍，并解决了基线从未成功的84个任务中的22个。结果在Mistral Large和Claude Sonnet 5上均成立，且一个模型构建的记忆可迁移给另一个模型使用并提升其表现。

Conclusion: 冻结模型并非必须在部署后停止学习；通过外部可检索的自然语言规则记忆，代理可以从普通运行反馈中持续改进，并具备跨模型迁移潜力。

Abstract: AI agents encounter learning opportunities in every episode they run, and discard nearly all of them: the underlying models are frozen at deployment, so an agent that resolves a difficult request today starts from zero when it recurs tomorrow. Yet ordinary operation already produces feedback, in the form of outcome verdicts and after-the-fact corrections. We show that this feedback is a sufficient signal for continual learning when the frozen model is paired with an external memory that distils each episode into retrievable natural-language rules. On the banking domain of $τ$-bench, against a static-RAG control retrieving over the complete policy corpus, learning from the one-bit outcome verdict lifts single-trial success to 1.6$\times$ the baseline, and learning from corrections to 2.6$\times$, converting 22 of the 84 tasks the baseline never solves. The result spans the deployment spectrum, measured on Mistral Large, an open-weights model that organisations with data sovereignty requirements can self-host, and replicated on a frontier model, Claude Sonnet 5. The accumulated memory also transfers: each model, reading the store built by the other, rises above its own no-memory baseline. The harness, protocol, and data are released.

</details>


### [122] [Deconstructing Off-Policy Ratios: Entropy-Scaled Trust Regions for Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22186)
*Guanqun Zhao,Zijun Xie,Binbin Zheng,Enlei Gong,Jiafeng Lu,Yehan Yang,Aoqi Hu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出熵缩放信任域（ESTR），用于异步强化学习下的大语言模型后训练，通过按 token 局部熵缩放离策略偏差，缓解陈旧数据导致的不稳定与策略崩溃，并在保持同步方法相近精度的同时显著提升训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 RL 能通过并行 rollout 生成与策略优化加速 LLM 后训练，但会产生陈旧的离策略数据。现有基于重要性比率幅值的统一阈值筛选方法忽略了不同 token 熵水平下重要性比率自然尺度的差异，可能错误保留噪声、抑制有效探索。

Method: 作者分析 token 熵与重要性比率尺度之间的系统关系，提出 Entropy-Scaled Trust Region（ESTR）：对每个 token 的离策略偏差按其局部熵进行缩放，以构造自适应信任域；该方法无需额外前向传播，也不需要显式检测模型版本切换。

Result: 在长程智能体任务和数学推理基准上，ESTR 稳定优于已有异步方法，并取得最佳的训练—推理一致性；相较同步 GRPO，ESTR 达到相近准确率，同时训练速度提升 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: 熵是异步 LLM 强化学习中校正离策略偏差的重要尺度因子。基于熵缩放的信任域能更合理地区分低熵噪声与高熵探索，从而提升异步训练的稳定性、效率与最终性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) accelerates large language model (LLM) post-training by overlapping rollout generation with policy optimization, but the resulting stale, off-policy data can destabilize optimization and ultimately cause policy collapse. Existing methods typically retain or discard tokens based solely on the magnitude of their importance ratios, applying the same threshold uniformly across token positions. In this work, we reveal that the natural scale of the importance ratio varies systematically with token entropy. Under asynchronous dynamics, this entropy-ratio scaling dictates two distinct phenomena: at low entropy, the inherent train-inference discrepancy is drastically amplified into substantial sampling noise; at high entropy, in-flight weight updates naturally induce pronounced, legitimate exploratory deviations. Consequently, magnitude-only correction inadvertently admits the amplified noise while strictly masking out the essential exploration triggered by in-flight updates. To address this, we propose the Entropy-Scaled Trust Region (ESTR), which scales each token's off-policy deviation by its local entropy, requiring no auxiliary forward passes or explicit version-switch detection. Across long-horizon agentic tasks and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ESTR consistently outperforms existing asynchronous methods and achieves the best train-inference consistency. Compared with synchronous GRPO, ESTR attains comparable accuracy while improving training speed by $2.6\times$.

</details>


### [123] [AI4PLE: A Methodology for Integrating AI into Product Line Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22260)
*Bedir Tekinerdogan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将人工智能系统化融入产品线工程（PLE）的 methodological framework，并通过工业环境中的多案例研究评估其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用式开发与产品线工程能够降低成本、提升质量并缩短上市时间，但PLE涉及特征选择、可变性管理、配置优化等复杂决策。目前人工智能在PLE流程中的系统化集成尚未被充分探索。

Method: 作者提出一个支持AI系统化集成到PLE过程中的方法框架，并在工业背景下开展多案例研究，对该框架的适用性和有效性进行评估。

Result: 摘要表明该框架在工业多案例研究中进行了有效性验证，但未提供具体量化结果或对比指标。

Conclusion: 该研究认为AI可通过结构化方法融入PLE，以辅助复杂决策并提升产品族创建、管理与定制效率；其贡献在于提供面向工业应用的系统化集成框架。

Abstract: Reuse-based development has become increasingly important in the creation of complex systems, offering significant opportunities to reduce costs, improve quality, and accelerate time-to-market. Product Line Engineering (PLE) provides a systematic approach to realizing this potential by enabling the efficient creation, management, and customization of product families by reusing shared assets and capabilities. PLE involves addressing numerous complex decisions, including feature selection, variability management, and configuration optimization, which are critical to the success of a product line. Despite its promise, the systematic integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI) into PLE processes has not yet been comprehensively explored. In this paper, we propose a methodological framework to support the systematic integration of AI into PLE and evaluate its effectiveness through a multi-case study conducted in an industrial context.

</details>


### [124] [A Roadmap to Impactful Pluralistic Alignment Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22305)
*Elinor Poole-Dayan,Jillian Fisher,Atoosa Kasirzadeh,Jacob Andreas,Mitchell Gordon,Michiel A. Bakker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，多元价值对齐虽已成为研究热点，但尚未明显影响主流生产级AI系统的训练与评估；作者呼吁研究从规范性论证转向可实证、可操作、可部署的方法与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元价值对齐的核心目标是让AI系统代表并服务于多样化的人类价值与观点，尤其是影响被数十亿用户使用的模型。然而作者发现，前沿实验室公开的行为文档和评测中几乎没有将“多元主义”明确作为目标，生产模型也缺乏被显式训练或测试以支持多元主义的公开证据。

Method: 论文采用审计与论证结合的方法：审查前沿AI实验室公开发布的模型行为规范、文档和评估材料，分析多元价值对齐在实际部署系统中的采用情况；随后归纳采用不足的原因，并提出对应的未来研究方向。

Result: 审计结果显示，当前公开资料中没有前沿实验室明确将多元主义命名为模型目标，也没有清晰证据表明生产模型被显式训练或评估以实现多元价值对齐。论文进一步总结出三类阻碍：缺乏实证收益证据、缺乏清晰可操作的理想多元行为定义、现有方法与生产系统需求之间存在未充分量化的权衡。

Conclusion: 作者认为，多元价值对齐研究若要产生真实影响，必须面向已部署且广泛使用的AI系统，建立经验性证据、明确可操作的目标，并发展能兼顾生产系统约束的评估与方法。

Abstract: Pluralistic value alignment---the goal of building AI systems that represent and serve diverse human values and perspectives---has emerged as an active research agenda. Yet, there's no public evidence that it has shaped the training or evaluation of the AI systems people actually use. We audit the public behavior documents and evaluations of frontier labs, finding none name pluralism as a goal, and as of this writing, no clear indication that production models are explicitly trained or tested for it. This goes against the primary motivations and goals of pluralistic alignment, which revolve around making a positive difference in the models serving billions of users worldwide. We argue that the pluralistic alignment research community should focus on supporting impact and adoption in deployed, widely-used AI systems. We provide evidence for the adoption problem, present three main reasons behind it, and discuss three corresponding areas for future research to address it: 1. The primary justifications for pluralistic alignment so far have been normative or speculative. We need studies showing empirically how pluralistic AI benefits users or society. 2. The pluralistic alignment research community has not settled when pluralistic behavior is warranted or what pluralism ideally looks like in practice. We need to establish a concrete goal for developers to operationalize. 3. Current methods trade off against other desiderata of LLMs in ways that are largely unmeasured, and existing metrics are not "hill-climbable." We need trade-off-aware evaluations and methods that meet the requirements of production systems. This paper serves as a collective call to action for the pluralistic alignment researchers: progress requires moving beyond normative justification toward empirical foundations, a concrete account of ideal pluralistic behavior, and practical methods and evaluations built for adoption.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Structural Convergence: A Neuro-Symbolic Benchmark for Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22365)
*Michael Romei De Socio,Gian Luca Pozzato,Alessio Merlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACTA 是一个用于复杂事件驱动系统中时间结构推理的受控合成基准，结果表明基于语义能力与上下文影响轨迹的时序学习优于原始事件级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高复杂度运行环境需要识别和预判跨时间分布的模式，而不仅是对孤立事件进行分类；因此需要评估模型在时间结构推理、早期预警和模式识别方面的能力。

Method: 论文提出 TRACTA 基准，包含 early_warning、pattern_detection 和 run_classification 三类任务；比较原始事件神经模型、contract-lite 语义基线，以及在语义化轨迹上运行的神经符号配置，并通过消融实验和捷径诊断分析不同信息来源与潜在浅层线索。

Result: 原始事件级学习仍具信息价值，但对语义能力轨迹和上下文直接影响轨迹进行时序建模取得最高总体点估计，尤其在时间相关任务上优势最大；消融显示能力动态、上下文影响和时间结构提供互补信息；主要神经输入视图中最直接的跨运行全局标识符捷径得到控制，但仍存在残余浅层信号。

Conclusion: 在受控合成环境中，语义化轨迹是进行时间结构推理的有效表示形式；该结果支持进一步研究事件数据、结构化表示与时间学习之间的语义接口，但结论范围应限于合成基准设定。

Abstract: High-complexity operational environments require methods that detect and anticipate temporally distributed patterns rather than classify isolated events. This paper introduces TRACTA (Temporal Reasoning and Capability-Trajectory Analysis), a controlled synthetic benchmark for temporal structural reasoning in high-complexity event-driven systems, instantiated through Multi-Domain Operations (MDO)-like scenarios. The benchmark includes three tasks: early_warning, pattern_detection, and run_classification, and compares raw-event neural models, a contract-lite semantic baseline, and a neuro-symbolic configuration operating on semantically grounded trajectories. Results show that raw event-level learning remains informative, but learned temporal modeling over semantic capability and contextual direct-impact trajectories achieves the highest aggregate point estimates, with the largest margins on the temporal tasks. Ablation analysis indicates that capability dynamics, contextual impacts, and temporal structure contribute complementary information. Shortcut diagnostics indicate that the most direct cross-run global-identifier shortcut is controlled in the primary neural input view, while residual shallow signals remain. Overall, the findings support a bounded methodological conclusion: in controlled synthetic settings, semantically grounded trajectories provide an effective representation for temporal structural reasoning, supporting further investigation of semantic interfaces between event data, structured representations, and temporal learning.

</details>


### [126] [Do Agent Benchmarks Measure Capability? Protocol Validity in the Age of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22368)
*Jiaqi Shao,Hanck Chen,Wei Zhang,Maxm Pan,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HackDetect，用于审计智能体基准测试中的协议有效性问题，识别并量化因捷径/奖励黑客导致的分数膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试用于支持能力声明，但智能体可能通过公开答案、评测工件、生成器结构、反馈操纵或无效评分路径等方式绕过预期能力要求；目前缺少统一流程来归因这些捷径并量化其影响。

Method: 作者形式化定义协议有效性，提出事后审计方法 HackDetect：识别暴露点，判断智能体如何利用该暴露，并评估由此产生的分数是否具有误导性；同时定义 Mislead gap，即利用漏洞得分与预期能力得分之差，用于量化分数膨胀。

Result: 作者审计了 15 个智能体基准中的 2,385 条轨迹，发现 Frontier Science 轨迹中 67.0%、AutoLab 任务中 66.7% 存在暴露和奖励黑客证据；成对比较显示分数膨胀为 0.45–1.00。

Conclusion: 基准测试报告不应仅给出分数，还应提供证据证明分数确实反映了预期能力；HackDetect 为发现和量化协议漏洞导致的误导性得分提供了统一审计框架。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks increasingly evaluate repository editing, web research, terminal use, and long-horizon interaction. Their scores support capability claims only when the evaluation protocol keeps the intended capability necessary for success. Recent reward-hacking benchmarks and system reports show that agents can instead recover public solutions, read evaluation artifacts, infer generator structure, manipulate feedback, or benefit from invalid scoring paths; existing responses do not provide a common procedure for attributing these shortcuts and quantifying their effect across benchmarks. We formulate protocol validity and introduce HackDetect, a post-hoc audit that identifies an exposure, determines how the agent used it, and assesses whether the resulting score is misleading. We quantify score inflation with the Mislead gap, defined as the exploit score minus the intended score. We audit 2,385 traces across 15 agent benchmarks and find evidence of exposures and reward hacking in 67.0% of Frontier Science traces and 66.7% of AutoLab tasks. Across paired comparisons, we measure score inflation of 0.45-1.00, showing that benchmark reports should provide evidence that scores reflect the intended capability.

</details>


### [127] [IDEAgent: Agentic Quality-Diversity Search for Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22375)
*Varun Gumma,Navonil Majumder,Soumitra Sinhahajari,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 IDEAgent，将科研想法生成建模为质量-多样性联合搜索，并用 Yield 指标评估满足质量阈值且彼此多样的最大想法集合；在 32 个主题上的实验显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的科研创意系统通常只优化质量或多样性之一，导致想法相互接近，或产生大量平凡、不严谨、不清晰的概念；因此需要同时兼顾高质量与高多样性的创意生成框架。

Method: 作者将研究构思定义为 Quality-Diversity 搜索任务，提出多智能体框架 IDEAgent，通过想法谱系管理演化过程；用多目标反馈进行定向修复与精炼以提升质量，并通过轻量级顺序记忆以及与已完成想法、历史祖先、被拒提案的显式比较来增强多样性。同时提出 Yield 指标来衡量满足质量阈值且互相多样的最大想法集合。

Result: 在覆盖计算机科学 8 个领域的 32 个主题上，IDEAgent 的 Yield 比最佳基线高 3.89 倍，并在非零 Yield 的主题数量上达到基线的 8 倍；质量改进分析表明，修复与精炼有助于提升逻辑严谨性和清晰度，同时保持非显而易见性。

Conclusion: 科研创意生成不应单独追求质量或多样性，而应作为二者联合优化的问题；IDEAgent 证明了基于 QD 搜索的多智能体谱系演化机制能更稳定地产生既高质量又多样化的研究想法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly automated the process of scientific discovery over the past few years. However, existing systems share one core limitation: they generate and optimize ideas independently for either Quality or Diversity. This often leads to the generation of ideas in close proximity to one another or to a large set of trivial, unsound, or unclear concepts. In this work, we instead argue that research ideation should be treated as a conjunction of both objectives and framed as a Quality-Diversity (QD) search. In line with this perspective, we introduce IDEAgent, a multi-agent framework that manages the evolution of ideas through lineages. We jointly drive Quality using multi-objective feedback for dedicated repair and refinement, while Diversity is achieved through lightweight sequential memory and explicit comparison against completed ideas, their historical ancestors, and rejected proposals. To systematically evaluate this QD conjunction, we develop Yield, a joint metric that computes the largest set of mutually diverse ideas that satisfy a predetermined quality threshold. Finally, through evaluations across 32 topics spanning 8 domains of Computer Science, we show that IDEAgent outperforms the best baseline by 3.89x on Yield, while achieving non-zero Yield on 8x more topics. We further corroborate these findings through an analysis of quality improvements, showing that repair and refinement are crucial for building logical rigor and clarity while preserving non-obviousness. To encourage future research on QD-search-based ideation, we open-source IDEAgent at https://github.com/declare-lab/IDEAgent.

</details>


### [128] [Agentic Root Cause Analysis through Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22385)
*Amaury Wei,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRCA 是一种零样本、基于智能体的工业异常根因分析框架，通过数字孪生与工具增强大语言模型结合，在无需故障标签训练的情况下进行可解释诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业系统异常根因诊断对安全运行至关重要，但现有流程高度依赖人工假设生成与证据收集；现有数据驱动方法又常因黑盒性强、依赖稀缺故障标注样本而难以部署。

Method: 提出 AgentRCA：利用数据驱动数字孪生建模正常系统动态，并结合工具增强的大语言模型，在推理阶段迭代收集统计证据、评估竞争性假设，并选择最能解释观测异常的物理故障。

Result: 在真实多相流设施和大规模化工厂数据上，AgentRCA 在不使用故障特定训练数据的条件下，达到与全监督基线相竞争的诊断性能，并生成透明推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 自主、假设驱动、证据支撑的智能体推理可作为可扩展工业根因分析的实用基础，兼顾零样本能力与可解释性。

Abstract: Diagnosing the root cause of anomalies is essential for safe industrial operation. Despite extensive sensor instrumentation, formulating hypotheses and gathering evidence remains a manual process, creating a major operational bottleneck. While existing data-driven approaches aim to automate this, two critical limitations restrict their deployment: their operate as black boxes unable to justify their diagnosis, and they require scarce labeled examples of faulty operation. To address this gap, we introduce AgentRCA, a zero-shot agentic framework for evidence-grounded root cause analysis. Rather than learning fault-specific mappings, AgentRCA performs inference-time reasoning by combining a data-driven digital twin (modeling normal system dynamics) with a tool-augmented large language model. The agent iteratively gathers statistical evidence, evaluates competing hypotheses, and identifies the physical fault that best explains the observed behavior. Evaluated on a real-world multiphase-flow facility and a large-scale chemical plant, AgentRCA achieves diagnostic performance competitive with fully supervised baselines without relying on fault-specific training. Crucially, it produces transparent reasoning traces that explicitly link observed symptoms to their underlying physical causes. These results establish autonomous hypothesis-driven reasoning as a practical foundation for scalable industrial root cause analysis.

</details>


### [129] [SceneActBench: Can Agents Act on the 3D Scenes They See?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22393)
*Yifei Zhao,Xiangxin Zhou,Wenhao Yang,Jiaqi Tang,Pu Jian,Huanjin Yao,Jiarui Yao,Haowei Lin,Chunchao Guo,Zhuo Chen,Wenkai Lyu,Jianzhu Ma,Xueqian Wang,Wenxi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SceneActBench 是一个面向 3D 场景中视觉条件动作能力的基准，用统一的 agent-environment 循环评估 VLM 智能体在多物体 3D 任务中的实际操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 基准多关注文本回答或单物体操作，无法充分评估 VLM agent 在完整、多物体 3D 场景中的行动能力；随着 VLM agent 越来越多地使用工具与 3D 环境交互，需要更贴近实际操作的评测框架。

Method: 提出 SceneActBench，覆盖五类 3D 任务。输入包括 PNG 图像或采样视频帧，以及在适用情况下提供的 3D 资产；agent 在统一固定的环境交互循环中执行动作。最终输出通过隐藏真值和任务特定的几何指标进行评估。该基准由 210 个源实例构建，形成 520 个任务案例，并包含成对输入条件。

Result: 在 11 种专有 VLM 配置上的评测显示，Overall 分数范围为 38.6 到 50.2，且没有模型能在所有任务上 consistently 表现良好。论文还进一步分析了失败出现的位置与形式。

Conclusion: SceneActBench 揭示了当前 VLM agent 在复杂多物体 3D 场景中执行视觉条件动作仍存在明显不足，说明仅依赖文本或单对象评测无法全面反映其真实 3D 操作能力。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents increasingly use tools to act on 3D scenes rather than only describe them. Existing 3D benchmarks score textual responses or single-object operations, leaving agent action on complete multi-object 3D scenes under evaluated. We present SceneActBench, a benchmark for visually conditioned action across five 3D tasks under a unified agent-environment loop. Given PNG images or sampled video frames and, where applicable, supplied 3D assets, an agent acts on a 3D environment. We evaluate each final output against hidden ground truth with task-specific geometric metrics. SceneActBench comprises five tasks built from 210 source instances, yielding 520 task cases including paired input conditions. Every task runs through one fixed agent loop to keep the comparison fair. Across eleven proprietary VLM configurations, Overall scores span 38.6-50.2, and none performs consistently well across tasks. We further analyse where and how failures manifest.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamic Capability Scoping for Enterprise AI Agents: A Synthetic Dataset and Three-Source Permission Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22445)
*Halil Burak Noyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态最小权限的企业 AI Agent 凭证范围控制架构，并发布一个用于评估该机制的合成企业任务数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI Agent 通常在配置时被授予静态、长期且覆盖面过广的凭证集合，导致持久过度授权，扩大攻击面。作者认为应在检测之前通过动态最小权限进行预防：若凭证不在 Agent 上下文中，就无法被滥用。

Method: 提出三源架构：角色权限上限、任务上下文分类器、由策略推导的权限组合禁止规则，形成分层主动防御；支持强制执行与仅观察模式。进一步构建包含 600 个企业任务提示的合成数据集，基于多部门公司策略，按 15 类工具权限标注最小所需权限；采用提示生成与权限标注分离的两阶段流程，并用人工复核样本验证一致性。

Result: 数据集在人审样本上的一致性较高：Cohen’s κ 从复核前 0.917 提升到复核后 0.967。数据集与策略迭代使权限上限违规从 46 降至 3，减少 93%。

Conclusion: 动态权限范围控制可作为企业 AI Agent 误用与失配的主动预防机制；合成任务数据不仅可用于评估动态授权机制，还能反向促进企业策略细化与改进。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents are typically granted static credential sets at configuration time, holding every tool the role might need for every task they perform. This persistent over-privilege expands the attack surface. We argue that capability scoping must follow a dynamic least-privilege principle and be treated as a prevention mechanism before a detection one. A credential that does not exist in an agent's context cannot be misused regardless of the agent's reasoning or evasion sophistication. We outline a three-source architecture instantiating this principle: role-based ceilings, a task-context classifier, and policy-derived combination prohibitions creating a layered proactive defense against LLM agent misalignment and misuse cases. The architecture supports both enforcing and observe-only deployment; the latter records agent permission requests inconsistent with task context, producing a behavioral signal usable in misalignment research.
  As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we contribute a synthetic dataset of 600 enterprise task prompts grounded in a multi-department company policy, labeled with minimum required permissions across a 15-permission tool-based taxonomy that maps directly to deployable credentials or enforceable guardrails. The dataset is constructed via a two-pass pipeline that separates prompt generation from permission labeling to avoid circularity, and is validated against a 60-record/688 decisions human-reviewed sample (Cohen's $κ= 0.917$ pre-review and $κ= 0.967$ post-review). Iterating between dataset and policy reduced ceiling violations from 46 to 3, a 93% reduction. This shows that synthetic prompt generation can drive policy refinement when the two are developed together. The dataset, environment specification, and generation pipeline are released to support evaluation of dynamic scoping mechanisms.

</details>


### [131] [TRACE-ROUTER: Task-Consistent and Adaptive Online Routing for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22465)
*Ritik Raj,Souvik Kundu,Sarbartha Banerjee,Dheemanth Joshi,Ishita Vohra,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-Router 将路由决策从单次 LLM 调用提升到任务级别：任务进入时只选择一次模型，并用任务终态奖励更新策略，从而改善智能体长流程中的准确率—延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI 部署中常需在不同成本、质量、延迟特性的 LLM 之间路由。现有方法多针对每次 LLM 调用独立决策，但智能体应用通常是长时程工作流，最终质量只由延迟出现的任务级结果衡量，导致逐调用路由难以把最终反馈正确归因到单个路由动作。

Method: 提出 TRACE-Router：一种任务级路由框架。它在任务准入时基于上下文多臂老虎机选择一个后端模型，并将该任务后续所有 LLM 调用固定到该模型；任务完成后，用同时考虑准确率和延迟的终态奖励更新路由策略。该方法利用延迟任务反馈自适应学习，无需显式估计任务复杂度。

Result: 在三个智能体基准上，TRACE-Router 持续改善准确率—延迟权衡，并取得非支配的 Pareto 前沿点。在 tau2-Bench 上，相比与其延迟匹配的单模型插值基线高出 7–8 个准确率点；在 Terminal-Bench 上，相比最强单模型基线准确率高 7.1 个点，同时延迟降低 36%。

Conclusion: 该摘要表明，将路由监督单位与任务级结果对齐比逐调用路由更适合长流程智能体应用。TRACE-Router 通过任务级固定模型选择和终态奖励学习，有效提升了多模型部署中的性能—效率折中。

Abstract: Routing to select large language models (LLMs) with different cost-quality trade-offs has become a fundamental deployment feature of enterprise AI. Existing routers, primarily make independent routing decisions for each LLM call. However, agentic applications execute as long-horizon workflows whose quality is determined only by a delayed, task-level outcome. This mismatch prevents per-call routers from correctly attributing feedback to individual routing decisions. Towards mitigating this, we present TRACE-Router, a task-level routing framework that aligns routing with the unit of supervision. TRACE-Router assigns each task to a model once at admission using a contextual bandit, pins all subsequent LLM calls to the selected backend, and updates its policy using the task's terminal reward, jointly accounting for accuracy and latency. By leveraging delayed task feedback, TRACE-Router learns routing policies that adapt to the workload while avoiding explicit task-complexity estimation. Across three agentic benchmarks, TRACE-Router consistently improves the accuracy-latency trade-off, achieving non-dominated Pareto frontier points. On tau2-Bench, it outperforms latency-matched interpolation between individual models by 7-8 accuracy points, while on Terminal-Bench it achieves 7.1 higher accuracy points than the strongest single model baseline with 36% lower latency.

</details>


### [132] [The Regression Tax: Decomposing Why Skills Help and Hurt LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22520)
*Darshan Tank,Baran Nama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出，给 LLM Agent 添加程序性技能不能只看平均任务成功率提升，因为技能也会导致原本能完成的任务失败；可靠性更依赖 grounding 与 verification，而非单纯程序步骤设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常用平均成功率衡量技能是否有效，但该指标掩盖了技能引入后的负面影响，即“回退”现象：无技能时成功、有技能后失败。作者希望更细粒度地分解技能带来的收益与损害。

Method: 作者在两个办公自动化基准和三种模型 harness stack 上进行了近 6000 次运行，对比有技能与无技能 Agent 的表现，并区分 regression 与 residual failure。同时通过轨迹分析识别失败原因，并修正评估伪影。

Result: 研究发现，技能间性能差异主要来自回退更少，而不是新增成功更多。作者归纳出三类回退机制：skill description osmosis、grounding displacement 和 verification displacement。持续失败也主要源于 grounding 与 verification 支持不足。

Conclusion: 程序性技能应通过收益与回退的分解评估，而非仅看总体平均提升。未来技能设计应避免三类回退模式，并加强输入理解、任务 grounding 和输出验证能力。

Abstract: Adding procedural skills to an LLM agent is typically evaluated by average improvement in task success. However, this metric hides an important cost: skills can also make agents worse. We measure both sides by comparing agents with and without skills across nearly 6,000 runs spanning two office automation benchmarks and three model harness stacks. This allows us to distinguish two outcomes. A regression is a task solved without skills but failed after skills are added. A residual failure is a task that fails both with and without skills. We find that regressions are substantial enough that the best performing skills outperform others primarily by regressing less, not by gaining more. We identify three causes of regression: (i) skill description osmosis, a skill changes an agent's behavior simply by being present in context, even when it is never invoked; (ii) grounding displacement, a skill's prescribed procedure overrides how the agent interprets its inputs; and (iii) verification displacement, where the procedure suppresses checks the agent would otherwise perform on its outputs. Analysing persistent failures reveals the same underlying pattern. Existing skills overemphasize procedural guidance the stage least often responsible for failure while under supporting grounding and verification, the dominant sources of remaining errors. After correcting evaluation artifacts and studying traces, we find many regressions and persistent failures recoverable through better grounding and verification. Procedural skills should be evaluated by decomposing their net effect into gains and regressions, not by aggregate improvement alone. We identify three regression modes skills should avoid, and find that reliability depends more on grounding and verification than on procedural skill choice.

</details>


### [133] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning for assisting Air Traffic Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22525)
*Anduel Mehmeti,Gabriella Gigante,Salvatore Venticinque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在空中交通管制这一安全关键场景中，将可解释性方法应用于强化学习智能体，以提升人类对AI决策的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险关键环境中的AI应用需要可靠的人机协作与自动化，而信任是部署AI系统的核心前提；信任又与AI系统的可解释性密切相关。深度学习和强化学习模型决策过程通常较难解释，因此有必要探索其在安全关键任务中的可解释方法。

Method: 研究使用一个简化的空中交通管制环境作为测试平台，训练强化学习智能体为航班选择替代航路以避开禁飞区域；随后采用显著性图作为初步可解释性技术，分析输入特征对智能体决策的影响程度。

Result: 训练得到的强化学习智能体能够在简化环境中进行避开禁飞区域的航路决策；显著性图可揭示哪些输入区域或特征对智能体决策贡献较大，为理解其行为提供初步依据。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，可解释性技术可用于分析安全关键场景中的强化学习决策过程，并为建立对AI驱动空中交通管制辅助系统的信任提供基础；但当前仍处于简化测试和初步解释阶段，需要进一步验证与扩展。

Abstract: To effectively integrate AI into high-stakes, critical environments such as healthcare, autonomous driving, and aviation--and to advance toward higher levels of automation and seamless human-AI collaboration--building trust in AI-driven solutions is essential. Trust, in turn, is closely linked to the explainability of AI systems. The rapid advancements in AI across various domains have underscored the challenges of establishing trust, raising increasing interest in AI explainability even more when applied to deep learning. In this context, the present work aims to explore the application of explainability techniques to Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms, specifically within the safety-critical domain of Air Traffic Control (ATC). Using a simplified ATC environment as an initial testbed, an intelligent agent is trained with a reinforcement learning algorithm to make decisions on alternative flight routes that avoid no-fly zones. As a preliminary explainability approach, a saliency map is employed, providing insights into the input features that most significantly influence the agent's decision-making process.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Joint Load Balancing and Transmit Power Control for Energy Efficiency Maximization in the Satellite-Cell-Free Massive MIMO Uplink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21860)
*Ngo Tran Anh Thu,Lo Hai Long,Le Duc Anh Vu,Trinh Van Chien,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究混合卫星-无蜂窝 Massive MIMO 系统，在非理想信道状态信息和实际用户关联约束下，联合优化用户关联与功率控制，以提升能效和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络需要非地面与地面基础设施的无缝融合，以实现泛在连接；但在 LEO 卫星与地面 AP 协同服务场景中，用户关联、信道不完美以及能效优化仍具有挑战。

Method: 作者建立多个 LEO 卫星与地面 AP 协同服务用户的混合系统模型，在空间相关 Rician 衰落信道下采用 MRC，推导上行遍历吞吐量闭式表达式；进一步构建联合用户关联与功率控制的能效优化问题，并提出改进差分进化算法在多项式时间内搜索可行解。

Result: 理论分析揭示了用户-卫星和用户-AP关联模式对频谱效率与速率公平性的影响；数值结果验证了分析，并表明混合架构可显著提升能效和网络吞吐量。

Conclusion: 混合卫星-地面无蜂窝 Massive MIMO 是提升下一代网络覆盖、吞吐量和能效的有效方案；所提出的 IDE 框架可为大规模场景提供可扩展的用户-卫星-AP关联指导。

Abstract: The seamless integration of non-terrestrial and terrestrial infrastructures is a key enabler for ubiquitous connectivity in next-generation (NG) wireless networks. We investigate a hybrid satellite-cell-free Massive MIMO system, where multiple low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellites jointly serve users in unison with terrestrial access points (APs) under realistic imperfect channel state information and practical user association constraints. We first derive closed-form expressions of the uplink ergodic throughput by exploiting maximum ratio combining (MRC) for transmission over spatially correlated Rician fading channels. Our analysis reveals the characteristic impact of both user-satellite and user-AP association patterns on both the spectral efficiency and rate-fairness achieved. We then formulate an energy efficiency optimization problem under joint user association and power control. Since the problems are inherently NP-hard due to the binary nature of the user-association variables, we develop an improved Differential Evolution (IDE) framework that efficiently explores the feasible solutions in polynomial time. Numerical results validate our analysis and show that the proposed hybrid scheme substantially improves energy efficiency and network throughput. For large-scale scenarios, the DE framework provides practical user-satellite-AP association guidelines, enabling scalable performance gains.

</details>


### [135] [Trade-off for Secure UAV-ISCC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21939)
*Hongjiang Lei,Jun He,Congke Jiang,Ki-Hong Park,Wenqian Shen,Liang Yang,Gaofeng Pan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该摘要研究无人机辅助的集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）系统中安全通信、雷达感知与计算能效之间的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统独立架构难以同时高效支持通信、感知和计算任务；ISCC通过资源共享与协同设计可提升整体效率，但多维性能指标之间存在耦合与冲突，需要刻画其边界与权衡关系。

Method: 构建UAV辅助ISCC系统，联合优化无人机三维轨迹、波束成形、用户调度与计算频率；分别以平均保密速率、感知速率和计算能效最大化为目标建立优化问题，并进一步采用归一化加权和方法联合协调三类性能指标。

Result: 工作给出了不同场景下安全通信、雷达估计/感知和计算能效的性能边界，并通过加权多目标优化揭示三者之间的可调权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为面向空中平台的ISCC系统提供了性能协同设计的理论依据，说明联合轨迹、通信、感知与计算资源优化是提升系统综合性能的关键。

Abstract: The integrated sensing, communication, and computing (ISCC) system overcomes the limitations of conventional standalone architectures. Through resource sharing and collaborative design, it dynamically optimizes and jointly enhances communication, sensing, and computing performance, thereby significantly improving overall system efficiency. This work investigates the performance trade-off among secure communication rate, radar estimation rate, and computational energy efficiency in an uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted ISCC system. By jointly optimizing the UAV's three-dimensional (3D) trajectory, beamforming, user scheduling, and computational frequency, three optimization problems are formulated to maximize the average secrecy rate, sensing rate, and computational energy efficiency, respectively, thus establishing the system's performance boundaries under diverse scenarios. On this basis, the trade-off among security, sensing, and computation is further explored with the goal of maximizing the normalized weighted sum of the three performance metrics, which provides a theoretical basis for the performance-coordinated design of aerial ISCC systems.

</details>


### [136] [Binary Cyclic Codes With Simultaneously Large Minimum Distances and Dual Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22137)
*Ziling Heng,Jiantao Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一批长度为 2^s-1、码率约为 1/2 的二元循环码，使其最小距离与对偶距离同时较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元循环码具有高效编译码优势，但已知码族中同时具备较大最小距离和较大对偶距离的构造较少，尤其是在维数接近长度一半且最小距离较大的情形。

Method: 针对偶数 s，提出新的二元 [2^s-1,k] 循环码构造，重点设计维数为 2^{s-1}±1 或 2^{s-1}+c（c 在约 ±5s/2 范围内）的码，并分析其最小距离及对偶距离下界。

Result: 部分构造得到 [2^s-1,2^{s-1}±1,d] 码，其 d·d^⊥ 下界接近 n 或 2n；另一些构造得到 [2^s-1,2^{s-1}+c,d] 码，也具有类似的距离乘积下界。多数新码相较同长度同维数已知二元循环码具有更大的最小距离，并发现若干参数优良的码。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了码率约 1/2 的二元循环码构造，在同时提升最小距离和对偶距离方面取得改进，部分结果达到或接近现有码表中的优秀参数。

Abstract: Binary cyclic codes are an important class of linear codes that admit highly efficient encoding and decoding algorithms. Constructing binary cyclic codes with favorable parameters has been an active research topic for several decades. In recent years, substantial progress has been made in the study of binary $[n,k,d]$ cyclic codes with $k$ close to $n/2$ and $d\geq \sqrt{n}$. Nevertheless, among the known codes of this type, only a few families simultaneously possess both large minimum distances and large dual distances. In this paper, for even $s$, we focus on new constructions of $[2^{s}-1,k]$ binary cyclic codes with rate approximately $1/2$, for which both the minimum distance $d$ and the dual distance $d^\perp$ are simultaneously large. Some of the constructions yield binary cyclic codes with parameters $[2^s-1,2^{s-1}\pm 1,d]$ such that the lower bounds of $d\cdot d^\perp$ are close to $n$ or $2n$. The other constructions yield binary cyclic codes with parameters $[2^s -1, 2^{s-1} + c, d]$ for some $ 1- \frac{5s}{2} \leq c \leq \frac{5s}{2} -1$ such that the lower bounds of $d\cdot d^\perp$ are close to $n$ or $2n$. Compared with the known binary cyclic codes of the same length and dimension, the majority of the binary cyclic codes we construct exhibit larger minimum distances. In particular, we obtain several codes with excellent parameters, which are verified against the Code Tables available at http://www.codetables.de/.

</details>


### [137] [Finite-Support Structure in i.i.d.-Constrained Capacity of Finite-Memory Poisson Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22234)
*Renzhi Yuan,Mugen Peng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限码间干扰的离散时间泊松信道在峰值与平均光强约束下的 i.i.d. 输入受限容量问题，并证明最优 i.i.d. 输入分布必为有限支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直检光通信链路中常同时存在多径记忆和信号相关散粒噪声，有限 ISI 泊松信道能自然刻画该场景；在实际光强约束下理解其 i.i.d. 受限容量及最优输入结构具有理论与工程意义。

Method: 作者直接基于由有限记忆信道诱导的连续状态隐马尔可夫输出过程的熵率进行分析：建立对所有可行 i.i.d. 输入分布一致成立的滤波遗忘估计，推导熵率的一阶变分，构造相应影响函数的全纯延拓，并结合 KKT 条件与超线性增长论证。

Result: 证明了在峰值和平均光强约束下，任何最大化平稳互信息率的 i.i.d. 输入分布均具有有限支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了有限 ISI 泊松信道 i.i.d. 受限容量最优输入离散且有限支撑的严格证明，并为具有记忆和信号相关噪声的光信道容量分析提供了新的熵率变分工具。

Abstract: Discrete-time Poisson channels with finite intersymbol interference provide a natural model for direct-detection optical links in which multipath memory and signal-dependent shot noise appear simultaneously. Under peak and average optical-intensity constraints, we study the independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.)-constrained capacity problem of such channels. We prove that every input distribution maximizing the stationary mutual information rate within the i.i.d. input class has finite support. The proof is carried out directly on the entropy rate of the continuous-state hidden Markov output process induced by the finite-memory channel. We first establish a filtering-forgetting estimate whose constants are uniform over all admissible i.i.d. input laws. We then derive the entropy-rate first variation, construct a holomorphic extension of the corresponding influence function, and combine the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker condition with a supralinear growth argument.

</details>


### [138] [Over-the-Air Interference Nulling Using Active RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22239)
*Junzhi Wang,Jun Sun,Limin Liao,Xiangbai Liao,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究主动RIS辅助双向K用户干扰信道实现满DoF的可行条件，给出所需反射单元数量与功率预算的紧缩尺度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集无线网络中的干扰严重限制系统性能；RIS被认为可在DoF意义下实现干扰消除。主动RIS相比被动RIS具备增益能力，但受到单元增益约束和总反射功率约束，因此需要明确其实现满DoF所需的资源规模。

Method: 将空中干扰置零建模为带耦合非线性约束的随机线性系统，利用高维凸几何工具推导实现满DoF所需反射单元数量的尺度阈值；进一步考虑每个反射单元的电路功耗和总功率预算，分析RIS发射功率与电路功率之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出主动RIS在双向K用户干扰信道中实现满DoF所需反射单元数量的紧阈值；在含电路功耗的总功率约束场景下，建立了总功率阈值及对应反射单元数量阈值；仿真验证理论结果。

Conclusion: 主动RIS能够在满足适当反射单元规模和功率预算条件下实现干扰自由传输；其性能受反射功率、电路功耗和反射单元数量的基本权衡制约。

Abstract: Interference fundamentally limits the performance of dense wireless networks, and reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have recently emerged as a promising means of enabling interference-free transmission in the Degrees-of-Freedom (DoF) sense. This paper investigates the feasibility of achieving full DoF in a two-way K-user interference channel-a canonical interference-limited setting-by employing an active RIS. Unlike its passive counterpart, an active RIS is subject to both per-element gain constraints and a total reflection-power constraint, which renders over-the-air interference nulling equivalent to solving a constrained random linear system with coupled nonlinear constraints. By leveraging tools from high-dimensional convex geometry, we derive a tight scaling threshold on the required number of reflecting elements (REs) for full-DoF transmission. We further extend the analysis to scenarios where each RE incurs circuit power consumption under a total power budget, leading to a fundamental tradeoff between RIS transmit power and circuit power. For this setting, we establish the thresholds for both the total power and the corresponding number of REs required to achieve interference-free transmission. Simulation results validate the theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [139] [Over-the-Air Interference Nulling Using Passive RIS for Two-Way K-User Interference Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22259)
*Junzhi Wang,Jun Sun,Limin Liao,Xiangbai Liao,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在双向 K 用户干扰信道中，借助被动 RIS 是否能在 DoF 意义下实现无干扰传输，并给出了所需 RIS 单元数的充分与必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线网络的核心瓶颈是干扰；RIS 可通过重构无线信道来缓解干扰。双向 K 用户干扰信道干扰最严重，因此作者希望回答被动 RIS 能否从自由度层面实现空中干扰中和。

Method: 论文将 RIS 相位设计问题建模到高维凸几何框架中，针对单位模约束和直达/反射链路强度差异，利用高维随机信道的集中现象推导条件；同时分析不完美 CSI 对可达 DoF 的影响。

Result: 结果表明，借助足够数量的 RIS 单元，可实现干扰自由 DoF；所需 RIS 单元数的充分条件与必要条件在阶数量级上一致。若 CSI 误差低于某一阈值，仍可保持干扰自由 DoF。仿真验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 被动 RIS 能够在双向 K 用户干扰信道中实现空中干扰中和；高维随机性和集中现象是突破单位模约束及链路强度不均衡的关键，且理论所给 RIS 规模条件具有阶意义上的紧致性。

Abstract: Interference constitutes the fundamental performance bottleneck in wireless networks. Meanwhile, reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) has emerged as a promising technique for interference mitigation by directly modifying wireless channels. In this paper, we are interested in the following problem: whether \textit{interference-free} transmission (in terms of Degree-of-Freedom, DoF) can be achieved with the aid of passive RIS in the two-way K-user interference channel, which is regarded as the most severely interfered network. We show that the answer is affirmative, i.e., interference in this network can be neutralized over the air. To accomplish this goal, two prominent challenges arise: i) the unit-modulus constraint on each RIS reflecting coefficient; ii) the significant disparity between the strengths of the direct and reflective channels. To address these challenges, we exploit the high-dimensional and random nature of wireless channels. Specifically, we cast the problem within a high-dimensional convex geometric framework, which enables us to leverage the ubiquitous \textit{concentration} phenomenon in high-dimensional spaces. Based on this framework, we establish both sufficient and necessary conditions on the required number of RIS elements to achieve interference-free DoF, which turns out to \textit{coincide} in order sense. Furthermore, we characterize the impact of imperfect channel state information (CSI) on the achievable DoF and show that interference-free DoF remains achievable if the CSI error is below a certain threshold. Simulation results validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [140] [On the Maximality of Additive Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22297)
*Tim Alderson*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将线性码的 ABS 几何模型推广到加性码，研究“可扩展”是否必然存在“加性扩展”，并证明该命题在若干小参数族中成立但一般不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性码中，Alderson–Gács 定理表明可扩展线性码存在保持线性结构的扩展。本文关注更一般的 GF(q)-线性、定义在 GF(q^m) 字母表上的加性码，试图理解线性情形中的扩展性结论是否仍然适用，以及几何模型中的完备性与码的极大性之间是否仍等价。

Method: 作者首先把 Alderson–Bruen–Silverman 模型推广到加性码，建立码与射影几何中平坦族/projective system of flats 的对应；随后用该几何模型刻画无加性扩展的加性码，即其关联平坦系统是完备的。接着分别对特定参数族进行证明，并构造反例：利用 scattered linear sets 构造平方 q 上的可扩展但无加性扩展的加性码，另用不同方法给出一个 (30,2,24)_{8/2} 反例。

Result: 主要结果包括：长度 n、码字数 q^{km}、字母表大小 q^m 的码存在 ABS 模型当且仅当其等价于非退化加性码；无加性扩展的加性码等价于关联平坦系统完备；对于 (n,2,d)_{9/3}、(n,2,d)_{4/2} 和 (n,3,d)_{4/2}，若加性码可扩展，则存在加性扩展；但一般情形下该结论失败，存在可扩展却无加性扩展的 properly additive codes。

Conclusion: 论文表明，加性码的扩展理论与线性码显著不同：几何完备性不再保证码的极大性，可扩展性也不必导致保持加性结构的扩展。作者最后提出猜想：对素数 p，所有可扩展的 (n,2,d)_{p^2/p}-码都应存在加性扩展。

Abstract: An additive $(n,k,d)_{q^m/q}$-code is a $\mathrm{GF}(q)$-linear subspace of $\mathrm{GF}(q^m)^n$ of $\mathrm{GF}(q)$-dimension $km$ with minimum Hamming distance $d$. We first extend the Alderson--Bruen--Silverman (ABS) model of linear codes to the additive setting: a code of length $n$ with $q^{km}$ words over an alphabet of size $q^m$ admits an ABS model if and only if it is equivalent to a nondegenerate additive code. We then ask whether an additive code that admits an extension must admit an \emph{additive} extension. For linear codes ($m=1$) this is a theorem of Alderson and Gács. We characterize the additive codes admitting no additive extension as those whose associated projective system of flats is complete, and we prove that the answer to the question above is again affirmative for $(n,2,d)_{9/3}$-, $(n,2,d)_{4/2}$-, and $(n,3,d)_{4/2}$-codes. In contrast with the linear case, we show that the answer is negative in general. Scattered linear sets yield, for each square $q$, extendable additive $(n,2,d)_{q^2/q}$-codes admitting no additive extension. Further, a different method yields an extendable additive $(30,2,24)_{8/2}$-code with no additive extension. Consequently, for properly additive codes, completeness of the associated projective system does not imply maximality of the code. We conjecture that extendable $(n,2,d)_{p^2/p}$-codes, $p$ prime, always admit additive extensions.

</details>


### [141] [Robust Berrut-Approximated Coded Computing via Discrete Cosine Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22427)
*Rimpi Borah,J. Harshan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 Robust Berrut Approximated Coded Computing（RBACC），将 BACC 与 DCT 码联系起来，使任意非多项式函数的分布式计算在存在慢节点和 Byzantine 错误节点时仍可进行误差定位、纠错与高精度重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 BACC 能通过有理插值支持任意非多项式函数的分布式编码计算，并具备抗慢节点能力和近似误差保证，但其面对 Byzantine 工作节点（返回错误结果的节点）时的鲁棒性尚未充分研究。

Method: 作者提出 RBACC 框架，通过新的评价点选择建立 Berrut 插值与离散余弦变换（DCT）码之间的联系，从而在有限精度计算下实现错误定位和错误纠正；同时推导不同场景下的近似误差上界，并构建优化问题以选择 DCT 码维度和分配编码计算任务给不可靠节点。

Result: 理论上，论文给出了仅有慢节点以及同时存在 Byzantine 节点和有限精度误差时的解析误差上界；实验上，RBACC 能有效缓解慢节点和错误节点影响，并相较基线方法获得更好的重构精度。

Conclusion: RBACC 为 BACC 提供了系统的编码理论鲁棒化扩展，将 DCT 码引入近似编码计算，在错误定位、纠错和参数优化方面提升了分布式非多项式函数计算的可靠性与精度。

Abstract: Coded computing is a reliable and fault-tolerant paradigm for executing large-scale computational tasks over distributed worker nodes. Among existing coded computing frameworks, Berrut Approximated Coded Computing (BACC) enables distributed computation of arbitrary non-polynomial functions through rational interpolation. Although BACC provides provable approximation guarantees and resilience against straggling workers, its robustness against Byzantine workers remains largely unexplored. To fill this research gap, we propose Robust Berrut Approximated Coded Computing (RBACC), which establishes a coding-theoretic framework for BACC by enabling error localization and error correction in the presence of Byzantine workers. In particular, RBACC introduces a new choice of evaluation points that establishes a connection between Berrut interpolation and Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) codes, thereby enabling error localization and error correction under finite-precision arithmetic. We derive analytical upper bounds on the approximation error of RBACC under multiple operating scenarios, including straggler-only systems and systems with Byzantine workers under finite-precision arithmetic. Building upon this analysis, we formulate several optimization problems for selecting the DCT code dimension and for assigning encoded evaluations to unreliable workers. We show that these are previously unexplored design parameters that can be systematically optimized to improve the reconstruction accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed RBACC framework effectively mitigates stragglers and Byzantine workers while offering improved reconstruction accuracy over the baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Microwave Linear Analog Computers (MiLACs) for Communications: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22509)
*Matteo Nerini,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出利用微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）在射频模拟域分担MIMO信号处理，以提升大规模天线系统的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线系统需要更大规模天线阵列和更复杂信号处理，传统全数字MIMO架构在RF链路数量、数据转换器精度、计算复杂度和能耗方面难以扩展，因此需要将部分计算从数字域迁移到模拟/RF域。

Method: 论文介绍并综述MiLAC：通过可调线性微波网络直接对通信射频信号进行矩阵运算。利用波传播的物理过程实现快速计算，并利用网络输出对可调参数的非线性依赖来实现矩阵求逆、伪逆等超出简单线性变换的操作；进一步讨论其在MIMO架构、零迫波束成形、RF链减少以及DAC/ADC要求放宽中的应用。

Result: MiLAC可将矩阵求逆和伪逆的复杂度从传统数字计算的三次量级降低到二次量级，对零迫波束成形尤为关键；同时有潜力减少RF链数量、降低DAC/ADC分辨率需求并减少波束成形计算负担。

Conclusion: MiLAC辅助MIMO是一种有前景的模拟-数字协同架构，可改善未来大规模无线系统的可扩展性，但仍需解决网络设计、可调性、精度、损耗、噪声、集成实现和系统级验证等关键挑战。

Abstract: Future wireless systems will require ever larger antenna arrays and heavier signal processing, making conventional digital multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures difficult to scale. In this paper, we show that a possible solution is to offload part of the processing from the digital to the analog domain. This can be done through linear microwave networks designed to compute directly using the communication signals at radio frequency (RF). These networks, denoted as microwave linear analog computers (MiLACs), can perform useful matrix operations instantly through wave propagation. Remarkably, although MiLACs are linear, the output signals can depend nonlinearly on the tunable parameters of the network, enabling the computation of operations beyond simple linear transforms. In particular, MiLACs can realize matrix inversion and pseudo-inversion with complexity scaling quadratically with matrix size, rather than cubically, which is essential in zero-forcing beamforming. We then review how MiLAC-aided MIMO architectures can reduce the number of RF chains, relax the resolution requirements on digital-to-analog converters (DACs) and analog-to-digital converters (ADCs), and decrease the beamforming complexity. We finally discuss the main challenges related to MiLAC and promising directions for future research.

</details>
